## Event counterparts: entity drops, portals, taming, teleports and other base entity hooks (#1014)

Fabric evidence is at `fabric-26.3/` pinned `2065040b236a0d5ac011bd6a45625dc49579b222`. Horizon call sites
are named by function. Scope: the twelve `base` hooks `entity_drop_item`, `entity_enter_block`,
`entity_enter_love_mode`, `entity_portal`, `entity_pose_change`, `entity_shoot_bow`, `entity_tame`,
`entity_teleport`, `entity_toggle_glide`, `entity_unleash`, `exp_bottle` and `pickup_xp`; all twelve record a
gap and no keys are added.

| Horizon hook | Fabric event considered / status | Evidence and porting boundary |
|---|---|---|
| `player_toggle_flight` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no flight-toggle event and the pinned index lists none: its only flight-shaped entries are the two `EntityElytraEvents`, which gate elytra gliding rather than a player's ability state. Horizon's `native_player_toggle_flight` is fired by `lan_flight_toggled` for a guest's requested start or stop of flying, with the player's id and name, the requested state and a `cancel` that reverts their abilities and resends them. Fabric has no such moment, arguments or veto. No key. |
| `anvil_damaged` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 has no anvil-damage event. Its nearest `BlockTransformerEvents.MODIFY` edits transformer definitions as they load ([BlockTransformerEvents.java:28-31,52-80][block-transformer-api]), dispatched by [BlockTransformerHelperImpl.java:49-67][base-player-transformer-impl]; it receives a transformer key, transformation list, resource source and registry lookup, not a damaged anvil in the world. Horizon's `native_anvil_damaged` is called by `anvil_used` after the 12% roll has already replaced the anvil state (`minecraft:anvil` → `chipped_anvil` → `damaged_anvil` → air), with the dimension, position, previous and new block names. No Fabric event fires after that random damage or carries both states. No key. |
| `arrow_loose` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no bow-release or projectile-launch event; `UseItemCallback.EVENT` fires at `ServerPlayerGameMode.useItem` HEAD ([ServerPlayerGameModeMixin.java:96-103][pbb-mixin]), when use is attempted, rather than on bow release; it supplies the player, level and hand, not mutable charge or ammo availability. Horizon's `native_arrow_loose` is fired by `release_bow` with the bow stack, whether ammo was found, a mutable charge in ticks and a `canceled` that suppresses the shot. The later `native_entity_shoot_bow` reports a spawned arrow, a different moment. No key. |
| `beacon_state_change` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 has no beacon activation event and no block-entity tick event; `ServerBlockEntityEvents` only reports a block entity loading or unloading, not a state change. Horizon's `native_beacon_state_change` is fired by `tick_beacon_at` when a beacon's pyramid level crosses between zero and non-zero, with the dimension, position and whether the beam is now active. No Fabric event fires at that comparison or carries `active`. No key. |
| `bell_ring` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 has no bell-ring event; `UseBlockCallback` and `BlockEvents.USE_WITHOUT_ITEM` fire at the start of a player's block use, before the bell's ring attempt, and never fire for a projectile ring. Horizon's `native_bell_ring` is fired by `allow_bell_ring` on an authoritative server with the dimension, the ringing player (optional) and the position, and its `cancel` stops the ring and raider reveal. No Fabric event fires at that point, with those arguments, or with that veto. No key. |
| `block_break_progress_update` | **Gap:** `PlayerBlockBreakEvents.BEFORE`/`AFTER`, `ClientPlayerBlockBreakEvents.AFTER` (a completed destroy, not a progress stage) | [pbb-api] lines 36-44 and 58-64 declare `BEFORE`/`AFTER`, fired by [pbb-mixin] lines 103-111 and 114-122 at `ServerPlayerGameMode.destroyBlock` around `playerWillDestroy` and `Block.destroy`; the client `AFTER` is fired by [interaction-client] lines 89-97 at the same destroy call. `BEFORE` can veto the impending break, while the two `AFTER` callbacks notify at the destroy call; none runs on each destroy-stage update or carries its numeric progress. Horizon's `native_block_break_progress_update` is fired by `guest_dig_feedback` whenever a guest's mining progress moves to a new stage (0-9), with the player's id and name, position and progress. No key. |
| `block_burn` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 has no block-burn event and no fire-tick hook; the pinned index's block events cover breaking, use and transformation, none of which fires when fire consumes a block. Horizon's `native_block_burn` is fired by `allow_block_burn` on an authoritative server with the dimension and position, and its `cancel` keeps the block. No Fabric event fires at that moment or offers that veto. No key. |
| `block_cook` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 has no cooking or smelting event; the pinned index lists no furnace, campfire or recipe-completion event, and NeoForge has no counterpart either. Horizon's `native_block_cook` is fired by `tick_campfire` after `cook_tick` returns finished items, with the position, the cooked stack's id and the result stack's id. No Fabric event fires on a completed cook or carries item data. No key. |
| `block_damage_abort` | **Gap:** `PlayerBlockBreakEvents.CANCELED`; nearest `AttackBlockCallback.EVENT` | [PlayerBlockBreakEvents.java:71-83][pbb-api] declares a server notification when a listener cancels a break; [ServerPlayerGameModeMixin.java:107-115][pbb-mixin] fires it only when `BEFORE` returns false, with player, level, position, state and block entity. The same mixin's lines 59-63 restrict `AttackBlockCallback.EVENT` to `START_DESTROY_BLOCK`, excluding abort packets. Horizon's `native_block_damage_abort` is fired by `lan_guest_packet` when a guest sends ABORT_DESTROY_BLOCK, after the server has cleared the dig and resent the block, with the player's id and name and the position. The trigger, firing point and arguments differ; a listener veto is not a player abandoning a dig. No key. |
| `block_dispense_armor` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 has no dispense event; the pinned index lists nothing at `DispenseItemBehavior` or `DefaultDispenseItemBehavior`, and NeoForge has none either. Horizon's `native_block_dispense_armor` is fired by `dispense_equipment` after a dispenser has equipped the item on a player, mob or armour stand, with the dimension, position, item id and target kind. Firing point, arguments and the completed equip have no Fabric counterpart. No key. |
| `block_fade` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 has no block-fade or random-block-tick event; neither the pinned index nor NeoForge has one. Horizon's `native_block_fade` is fired by `allow_block_fade` on an authoritative server with the dimension, position, the `from` and `to` state ids and a `cancel` that keeps the block. No Fabric event fires at that state transition or carries both states. No key. |
| `block_failed_dispense` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 has no dispenser event, failed or otherwise; the pinned index lists nothing at `DispenseItemBehavior.dispense`. Horizon's `native_block_failed_dispense` is fired by `dispense_item` after a mod's dispense answer is `FAIL`, with the dimension, position and item id. No Fabric event fires on a failed dispense or carries the item. No key. |

## Event counterparts: base mending, explosions, fire, furnaces and hoppers (#1014)

are named by function. Scope: the twelve hooks `xp_repair_ratio`, `explosion_prime`, `fire_odds`,
`smoke_source`, `firework_explode`, `fluid_level_change`, `framerate_limit`, `furnace_burn`,
`furnace_extract`, `furnace_smelt`, `furnace_start_smelt` and `hopper_inventory_search`; all twelve
record a gap and no keys are added.

| Horizon hook | Fabric event considered / status | Evidence and porting boundary |
|---|---|---|
| `block_form` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no event for precipitation forming ice or placing or thickening snow, and the pinned index lists nothing at that block-state transition. Horizon's `native_block_form` is fired by `allow_block_form` on an authoritative server with the dimension, position and the `from`/`to` state ids, and its `cancel` keeps the old state. No Fabric event fires at that moment or carries both states. No key. |
| `block_ignite` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 has no block-ignite event; the pinned index lists nothing at fire placement by flint and steel, fire charge, lava, lightning or fire spread. Horizon's `native_block_ignite` is fired by `allow_block_ignite` on an authoritative server with the dimension, the acting player's id (optional), the position and a cause string, and its `cancel` leaves the position unchanged. No Fabric event fires at that moment or carries the cause. No key. |
| `block_lock_check` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 has no guest block-inventory lock check; its container-screen mixins and screen events cover client menu rendering, not a server-side locked-container decision. Horizon's `native_block_lock_check` is fired by `open_block_menu_with_opener` for each half of a guest's requested container, with the guest's id and name, the position and the mutable vanilla `locked` result. That is a Horizon LAN guest-state query with no Fabric firing point or arguments. No key. |
| `block_receive_game` | **Gap:** none; nearest `ClientReceiveMessageEvents.ALLOW_GAME` | The dossier's only name candidates are client chat/overlay events: [receive-api] lines 57-71 declares `ALLOW_GAME` and [receive-mixin] lines 66-85 fires it at `ChatListener.handleSystemMessage` and `handleOverlay` HEAD, carrying a message component and an overlay flag, not a sculk sensor's game event. Fabric API 26.3 declares no sculk or vibration event and the pinned index lists none. Horizon's `native_block_receive_game` is fired by `sculk_receive` with the dimension, position, receiving block id, the vibration's event id and a `cancel` that suppresses the response. Firing point, arguments and side differ. No key. |
| `block_shear_entity` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 has no dispense or shear event; the pinned index lists nothing at a dispenser shearing an entity. Horizon's `native_block_shear_entity` is fired by `shear_entity` after the dispenser has applied the shear effects, with the dimension, the target block position in front of the dispenser, the sheared entity's id and its kind. No Fabric event fires after that shear or carries the entity. No key. |
| `brewing_complete`, `brewing_fuel`, `brewing_start` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no brewing-stand event; the pinned index lists no brewing, potion or fuel event, and the dossier's `ClientConfigurationConnectionEvents.COMPLETE` candidate only reports a client finishing configuration. Horizon's `native_brewing_complete`, `native_brewing_fuel` and `native_brewing_start` are fired by `tick_brewing_stand` after `brew_after_pre` reports a finished brew (position), when fuel is consumed (with the fuel item id) and when brewing begins (with the ingredient id). No Fabric event fires at any of those moments or carries those item ids. No keys. |
| `broadcast_message` | **Gap:** `ServerMessageEvents.ALLOW_CHAT_MESSAGE`, `ALLOW_COMMAND_MESSAGE`, `ALLOW_GAME_MESSAGE`; nearest `ServerMessageEvents.GAME_MESSAGE` | [smsg-api] lines 33-46, 54-63 and 71-84 declare three separate veto-only events, all fired at `PlayerList` HEAD by [player-list-mixin] lines 44-51, 54-61 and 64-86 for player chat, system messages and command messages respectively (player commands also trigger the chat event after the command event); each takes a `PlayerChatMessage` with a player or command source and chat type, or a server/component/overlay triple, and returning false drops the message. Fabric splits one generic broadcast into three moments and offers no way to edit the component. Horizon's `native_broadcast_message` is fired by `native_broadcast` for any server broadcast line with a mutable `Text` and a `cancel`, and the possibly edited line is logged after the hook. Firing point, scope, mutable line and veto/mutation behaviour differ. No key. |
| `campfire_start` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 has no campfire event and no cooking-start event; the pinned index lists none. Horizon's `native_campfire_start` is fired by `use_on_campfire` after `place_food` accepted the held stack, with the position and the item id, and the item is then consumed. No Fabric event fires on that placement or carries the item. No key. |
| `cauldron_level_changed` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 has no cauldron event; the pinned index lists none, and the hook's own `none (Bukkit's CauldronLevelChangeEvent)` note records the same for NeoForge. Horizon's `native_cauldron_level_changed` is fired by `allow_cauldron_level_change` on an authoritative server with the dimension, the acting player (optional), the position, the from/to levels, a reason string and a `cancel` that leaves the cauldron unchanged. No Fabric event fires at that fill-level change or carries those levels. No key. |
| `item_consumption_teleport` | **Gap:** `ServerEntityLevelChangeEvents.AFTER_ENTITY_CHANGE_LEVEL` / `AFTER_PLAYER_CHANGE_LEVEL` (nearest) | [ServerEntityLevelChangeEvents.java:32-46,49-63][level-change-api] declares separate non-player and player notifications: [EntityMixin.java:37-44][base-block-entity-mixin] fires the former only after a non-null cross-dimension teleport result, with the original and new entities and both levels; [ServerPlayerMixin.java:84-86][base-block-server-player-mixin] fires the latter at `triggerDimensionChangeTriggers` TAIL with the player and both levels. Neither carries a mutable destination or veto, and the pinned index lists no same-level random-teleport event. Horizon's `native_item_consumption_teleport` is fired by `teleport_allowed` once per chorus-fruit attempt with the player's id and name, their feet position and a mutable target, and its `cancel` ends the effect. Firing point, mutable target and veto differ. No key. |
| `compost_item` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 has no composter event and the pinned index lists nothing at a compost layer being added. Horizon's `native_compost_item` is fired by `use_composter` after attempting `composter_add`, before the stat award and held-item consumption, with the position and item id; the call does not depend on the addition succeeding. No Fabric event covers that moment and argument contract. No key. |
| `crafter_craft` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 has no crafter event and the pinned index lists nothing at a crafter completing a recipe. Horizon's `native_crafter_craft` is fired by `crafter_dispense` after the grid inputs were consumed and the result ejected, with the position and the recipe id. No Fabric event fires at that moment or carries the recipe. No key. |
| `creeper_ignite` | **Gap:** none; nearest `UseEntityCallback.EVENT` | [bc-use-entity-api] lines 31-70 declares an interaction callback with player, level, hand, target entity and hit result; [bc-use-entity-mixin] lines 83-93 fires it before vanilla handles the interaction, with PASS continuing and any other result stopping processing on the server. Horizon's `native_creeper_ignite` is fired by `interact_mob` after the creeper was ignited, with the entity id, player uuid and item id. Firing point, object-versus-id arguments and cancellation behaviour differ. No key. |
| `creeper_power` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 has no lightning-charge or powered-creeper event, and the pinned index lists nothing at `Creeper.thunderHit`. Horizon's `native_creeper_power` is fired by `thunder_hit` for each powered creeper, with the entity id and strike position. No Fabric event fires at that moment. No key. |
| `critical_hit` | **Gap:** `AttackEntityCallback.EVENT`, `ServerLivingEntityEvents.ALLOW_DAMAGE` (nearest) | [bc-attack-entity-api] line 41 declares the attack callback, fired at `Player.attack` HEAD by [bc-player-mixin] lines 34-42 with the player, level, hand and target and able to stop further server processing with any non-PASS result (PASS continues); its hit-result argument is null there and it carries no critical decision or amount. [bc-living-api] line 36 declares `ALLOW_DAMAGE`, fired inside `hurtServer` by [bc-living-mixin] line 82, a veto over damage that is already computed. Neither exposes vanilla's critical answer, a mutable critical flag or a damage multiplier, and neither can force a crit as Horizon's hook can. Horizon's `native_critical_hit` is fired by `melee_damage` (and `legacy_melee_damage`) with the target kind, vanilla's answer and mutable `critical`/`damage_multiplier`. No key. |
| `difficulty_changed` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 has no difficulty-change event and the pinned index lists nothing at `MinecraftServer.setDifficulty`. Horizon's `native_difficulty_changed` is fired by `set_difficulty_forced` after the difficulty was written to the client state and level data and broadcast to players, with the old and new names. No Fabric event fires at that moment. No key. |
| `dragon_egg_form` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 has no dragon-fight or dragon-egg event, and the pinned index lists nothing after the dragon's death. Horizon's `native_dragon_egg_form` is fired by `dragon_killed`, only when the fight had not been completed before, with the dimension, the chosen position and a `cancel` that leaves the block unset. No Fabric event fires before that placement or carries a veto. No key. |
| `enchant_item` | **Gap:** `EnchantmentEvents.ALLOW_ENCHANTING` (nearest) | [bc-enchant-api] lines 54-67 declare a per-enchantment tri-state that answers whether an enchantment may apply to an item, invoked by `FabricItemStack.canBeEnchantedWith` at [bc-item-stack-api] lines 59-66; it carries no player, position, finished stack or charged levels, and allows nothing after the fact. Horizon's `native_enchant_item` is fired by `lan_enchant` after the offer was applied and the lapis and levels were paid, with the player's uuid and name, the position, the finished stack and the levels charged. Firing point, arguments and direction differ. No key. |
| `ender_pearl_teleport` | **Gap:** `ServerEntityLevelChangeEvents.AFTER_ENTITY_CHANGE_LEVEL` / `AFTER_PLAYER_CHANGE_LEVEL` (nearest) | [level-change-api] lines 32-46 and 49-63 declare only notifications after a cross-dimension change, fired by [base-block-entity-mixin] lines 37-44 and [base-block-server-player-mixin] lines 84-86 with the original and resulting non-player entities or the player, and both levels. Horizon's `native_ender_pearl_teleport` is fired on the pearl hit path for a server player with the pearl and player ids, their feet position, a mutable `target` and `damage`, and a `cancel` that keeps the player in place. Horizon's `native_exp_bottle` is fired by `splash` for an experience bottle after the 3-to-11 value was rolled and the orbs spawned, with the position and amount. No Fabric event fires at that moment or carries the amount. No key. |
| `pickup_xp` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no experience-orb pickup event, and the pinned index lists nothing at `ExperienceOrb.playerTouch`; `PlayerXpEvent.PickupXp` is NeoForge's. Horizon's `native_pickup_xp` is fired by `guest_orbs` for a touching orb, with the player uuid, dimension, orb id, mutable value and count, and a cancel that leaves the orb and its pickup delay unchanged. No Fabric event fires at that query or offers that veto. No key. |
| `xp_repair_ratio` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no Mending or experience-repair event or item extension, and the pinned index lists nothing for the `minecraft:repair_with_xp` repair; `IItemExtension.getXpRepairRatio` is NeoForge's. Horizon's `native_xp_repair_ratio` is fired by `mending` on a serving or dedicated host for the damaged Mending stack it picked, with the player id, the stack, the experience and a mutable ratio arriving as the `minecraft:repair_with_xp` repair per point; only the parts attached to the stack's item hear it. No Fabric event fires at that moment or carries the stack and ratio. No key. |
| `explosion_prime` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no explosion-prime or explosion-start event, and the pinned index lists nothing for an explosion beginning. Horizon's `native_explosion_prime` is fired by `server_explode_as` after `native_explosion_start` was not cancelled and before the blast gathers entities, with the centre and radius only. No Fabric event fires at that moment. No key. |
| `fire_odds` | **Gap:** none; nearest `FlammableBlockRegistry` | Fabric has no query-time flammability event: [the flammable registry][flammable-registry] lines 26-40 expose a block/tag-to-entry registry, and [the fire block hooks][fire-block-hooks] line 24 only exposes the vanilla entry for a state, with no level, position or per-call mod callback. Horizon's `native_fire_odds` is fired by `before_fire_odds` on an authoritative server with the dimension, the state id, the block's position, the face toward the fire and a mutable ignite/burn pair, keyed by state rather than block. Registered data instead of an event at the query. No key. |
| `smoke_source` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no campfire or smoke-source event, and the pinned index lists nothing for `CampfireBlock.isSmokeSource`. Horizon's `native_smoke_source` is fired by `before_smoke_source` on an authoritative server with the state id and a mutable answer as the campfire is placed or the block under it changes. No Fabric event fires at that query. No key. |
| `firework_explode` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no firework event, and the pinned index lists nothing for a rocket's explosion. Horizon's `native_firework_explode` is fired by `explode_rocket` after the burst's damage was applied and its particles were queued, with the rocket's entity id, position and burst count. No Fabric event fires at that moment. No key. |
| `fluid_level_change` | **Gap:** `FluidFlowEvents.ALLOW` (nearest) | [The fluid flow events][fluid-flow-api] lines 30-38 declare `ALLOW`, a veto whose listener receives the fluid state, level and the position flowed into (line 62-71), fired by [the flowing fluid mixin][fluid-flow-mixin] lines 40-48 and [the lava fluid mixin][lava-fluid-mixin] lines 36-44 at `spreadTo` HEAD, plus [the liquid block mixin][liquid-block-mixin] lines 39-43 at `shouldSpreadLiquid` HEAD for neighbor updates. Horizon's `native_fluid_level_change` is fired by `allow_fluid_level_change` when a flowing fluid is about to change level, with the fluid name, position and both from/to levels, and cancelling keeps the old level. `ALLOW` observes spread attempts and neighbor updates, rather than the old-to-new level decision, and supplies a `FluidState` rather than the explicit old/new level pair. Firing point and arguments differ. No key. |
| `framerate_limit` | **Gap:** none | `FramerateLimitTracker.getFramerateLimit` fires no event, and Fabric API 26.3 declares no framerate or window event, which the pinned index confirms. Horizon's `native_framerate_limit` is fired by `framerate_limit` after Max Framerate, the inactivity throttle and Battery Mode were applied, with the focused, minimized and in-world flags and a mutable limit a mod may only lower. No Fabric event fires at that moment. No key. |
| `furnace_burn` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no furnace burn or fuel-consumed event, and the pinned index lists none. Horizon's `native_furnace_burn` is fired by `tick_furnace` after the block entity tick when fuel was consumed and the furnace is lit, with the block position and the fuel item id. No Fabric event fires at that moment. No key. |
| `furnace_extract` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no furnace-output extraction event, and the pinned index lists none. Horizon's `native_furnace_extract` is fired by `lan_machine_click` for a LAN guest whose click lowered the furnace output slot, only when someone listens, with the player uuid, name, block position, output item id and count taken. No Fabric event fires at that moment or carries the taker. No key. |
| `furnace_smelt` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no smelting-complete event, and the pinned index lists none. Horizon's `native_furnace_smelt` is fired by `tick_furnace` when the output slot's count grew, with the position, the input item id and the result id. No Fabric event fires at that moment. No key. |
| `furnace_start_smelt` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no event for cooking starting, and the pinned index lists none. Horizon's `native_furnace_start_smelt` is fired by `tick_furnace` when the cooking timer advanced from zero, with the position and the input item id. No Fabric event fires at that moment. No key. |
| `hopper_inventory_search` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no hopper or inventory-search event, and the pinned index lists none; the pinned tree's only hopper mixin is the transfer module's item-storage lookup, not an event. Horizon's `native_hopper_inventory_search` is fired by `hopper_eject` and `suck_from_container` once a container or entity target was selected, with the hopper position and exactly one of the container position or entity id. No Fabric event fires at that decision. No key. |
## Event counterparts: base hopper, inventory, item and block hooks (#1014)

Fabric evidence is at `fabric-26.3/` pinned `2065040b236a0d5ac011bd6a45625dc49579b222`. Horizon call sites
are named by function. Scope: the twelve `base` hooks `hopper_pickup_item`, `ingredient`,
`inventory_block_start`, `inventory_move_item`, `item_crafted`, `item_despawn`, `item_expire`, `item_spawn`,
`leaves_decay`, `lightning_strike`, `menu_quick_move` and `moisture_change`; all twelve record a gap and no
keys are added.

| Horizon hook | Fabric event considered / status | Evidence and porting boundary |
|---|---|---|
| `hopper_pickup_item` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no hopper-pickup or item-collection event, and the pinned index lists nothing for `HopperBlockEntity` taking a stack out of the level. Horizon's `native_hopper_pickup_item` is fired by `allow_hopper_pickup_item` on an authoritative server with the dimension, hopper position, item id, count and a mutable cancel that leaves the dropped stack in the world. No Fabric event fires at that moment or carries the item and count. No key. |
| `ingredient` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no per-occurrence custom recipe-ingredient data-load event, and the pinned event index lists none. Horizon's `native_ingredient` is called by `resolve` for each custom-ingredient occurrence with the load context and the ingredient JSON, and fills the matched item ids. No Fabric event fires at that moment with that collection contract. No key. |
| `inventory_block_start` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no server menu-open event, and the pinned index lists no server inventory-open callback. Horizon's `native_inventory_block_start` is fired by `open_block_menu_with_opener` after the menu-type and open-permission checks, with the player uuid, block-usage name and position, and cancelling refuses the open. No Fabric event fires at that moment or vetoes it. No key. |
| `inventory_move_item` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no per-move hopper or container event; the item transfer API is a storage abstraction with no per-transfer callback, and the pinned index lists none. Horizon's `native_inventory_move_item` is fired by `hopper_move_canceled` and `part_move_canceled` with the source and destination block positions, the item id and a mutable cancel. Firing point and arguments differ. No key. |
| `item_crafted` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no crafted-item event (`PlayerEvent.ItemCraftedEvent` is NeoForge's), and the pinned index lists nothing for `ResultSlot.onTake`. Horizon's `native_item_crafted` is fired by `click_inventory` after the take already happened, and by `guest_menu_op` for a LAN guest, with the player uuid, recipe id, taken stack and the grid's inputs before the take. No Fabric event fires at that moment. No key. |
| `item_despawn` | **Gap:** `ServerEntityEvents.ENTITY_UNLOAD` (nearest) | [The server entity event][sentity-api] lines 62-71 reports unloading with an `Entity` and `ServerLevel`, fired by [the entity callbacks mixin][sentity-callbacks-mixin] lines 43-45 at `onTrackingEnd` HEAD, including level shutdown via [the lifecycle implementation][hopper-lifecycle-impl] lines 49-60. Horizon's `native_item_despawn` is fired by `tick_dropped_items` after the age check when no listener extended the item's life and the host serves, with the entity id, item id, count and position. Fabric covers general unloading rather than the specific lifetime decision and supplies objects rather than the hook's ids and values. No key. |
| `item_expire` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no item-lifetime event (`ItemExpireEvent` is NeoForge's), and the pinned index lists none. Horizon's `native_item_expire` is fired by `tick_dropped_items` at the same age check with the entity id, stack and a mutable extra-life in ticks, capped at 32766. No Fabric event fires at that moment or accepts a lifetime extension. No key. |
| `item_spawn` | **Gap:** `ServerEntityEvents.ENTITY_LOAD` (nearest) | [The server entity event][sentity-api] lines 35-47 reports an entity already loaded into a server level, fired by [the entity callbacks mixin][sentity-callbacks-mixin] lines 38-40 at `onTrackingStart` TAIL; this is level tracking, not a player beginning to track it. Horizon's `native_item_spawn` is fired by `spawn_item` after the drop was added, with the entity id, item id, count and position. Fabric passes an `Entity` and `ServerLevel` rather than those ids and values, and also covers loaded entities rather than only newly created drops. Arguments and scope differ. No key. |
| `leaves_decay` | **Gap:** none; nearest `ServerPlayerEvents.LEAVE` (name only) | That event is a player disconnect: [the server player events][sp-api] lines 63-69 declare `LEAVE`, fired from `PlayerListMixin.remove` at HEAD ([sp-mixin] lines 47-55). Horizon's `native_leaves_decay` is fired by `allow_leaves_decay` on an authoritative server with the dimension and leaf position, and cancelling keeps the block. No Fabric event fires at the decay decision. No key. |
| `lightning_strike` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no lightning or weather-strike event, and the pinned index lists nothing for a bolt being created. Horizon's `native_lightning_strike` is fired by `lightning_strike_allowed` with the optional responsible player, block position, cause string (`weather`, `trident`, `command` or `mod`) and a mutable cancel that prevents the bolt and its effects. No Fabric event fires at that moment or carries a cause. No key. |
| `menu_quick_move` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no `AbstractContainerMenu.quickMoveStack` event; a Java menu overrides the method instead, and the pinned index lists no menu-move callback. Horizon's `native_menu_quick_move` is fired by `api_menu_quick_move` for a mod block entity's menu with the position, container slot count, slot index, a mutable stack and a handled flag that skips vanilla routing. No Fabric event fires at that routing decision or provides that mutable stack and handled contract. No key. |
| `moisture_change` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no farmland-moisture event, and the pinned index lists nothing for the farmland tick that changes moisture. Horizon's `native_moisture_change` is fired by `allow_moisture_change` on an authoritative server with the dimension, position, old and new moisture, and cancelling keeps the old value. No Fabric event fires at that decision. No key. |

The change is docs only: no keys, dispatch, tick-time work, adapter, alias or gameplay
change.

## Event counterparts: base anvil, armor-stand, bucket, menu and player hooks (#1014)

Fabric evidence is at `fabric-26.3/` pinned `2065040b236a0d5ac011bd6a45625dc49579b222`. Horizon call sites are named
by function. Scope: the eleven `base` hooks `anvil_craft_post`, `anvil_craft_pre`, `armor_stand_manipulate`,
`bucket_empty`, `container_opened`, `creative_slot`, `food_level_changed`, `hanging_break`, `hanging_place`,
`player_changed_dimension` and `player_clone`; all eleven record a gap and no keys are added.

| Horizon hook | Fabric event considered / status | Evidence and porting boundary |
|---|---|---|
| `anvil_craft_post` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no anvil-take event and the pinned index lists nothing for the anvil menu; its creative-tab and item-click events only edit tab contents or handle slot clicks, neither at this moment. Horizon's `Game::native_anvil_craft_post` (from `Game::api_anvil_craft_post`) passes the player uuid, name, anvil position and the `AnvilCraft` inputs and result, after a take the pre hook did not cancel. No Fabric event fires there or carries that data. No key. |
| `anvil_craft_pre` | **Gap:** none | The same absence: Fabric has no event before an anvil take. Horizon's `Game::native_anvil_craft_pre` (from `Game::api_anvil_craft_pre`) fires before the levels are charged and the inputs used, with the `AnvilCraft` and a mutable cancel that leaves the result in its slot. No Fabric event fires at that point or vetoes the take. No key. |
| `armor_stand_manipulate` | **Gap:** none; nearest `UseEntityCallback.EVENT` (different point and arguments) | [Use entity API][use-entity-api] lines 29–67 and [the server packet mixin][use-entity-server-mixin] lines 83–92 expose the player, level, hand, entity and hit result before the entity interaction. Fabric also runs on the client; on the server, `PASS` continues and any other result cancels. Horizon's `interact_armor_stand` fires only after the stand's disabled-slot checks and `swapItem` locks, only when the click moves something, and passes the stand id and slot name with a cancel. Point, side coverage and arguments differ; a server veto alone does not establish equivalence. No key. |
| `bucket_empty` | **Gap:** none; nearest `UseItemCallback.EVENT` (before `Item.use`) | [Use item API][use-item-api] lines 28–54 and [the server game-mode mixin][interaction-server] lines 96–103 fire at `useItem` HEAD, before the item is used, with the player, level and hand; `PASS` continues and other results bypass vanilla. Horizon's `bucket_empty_cancelled` runs inside `BucketItem.use`, after the destination block and the build permission are known, with the position and fluid id and a cancel that leaves the bucket full. Moment and arguments differ. No key. |
| `container_opened` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no server menu-open event and the pinned index lists nothing for `ServerPlayer.openMenu`; Horizon's cancellable opening hook is not the counterpart of NeoForge's `PlayerContainerEvent.Open`. Horizon's `guest_may_open` fires after vanilla's refusal checks and before the menu that was open closes, with the menu kind, optional block position and a cancel. No Fabric event fires at that moment or vetoes the open. No key. |
| `creative_slot` | **Gap:** none; nearest `CreativeModeTabEvents` (different moment) | Those events only edit a tab's entries while its contents are built, and never see `ServerGamePacketListenerImpl.handleSetCreativeModeSlot`. Horizon's `guest_may_set_creative_slot` fires once the packet passed vanilla's checks, with the slot index, item id, count and a cancel that drops the packet. Point and arguments differ. No key. |
| `food_level_changed` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no hunger or food-level event and the pinned index lists nothing for `FoodData`. Horizon's `leave_guest` fires it once per guest turn, after that turn's eating, hunger and starving changes, with the uuid, name, old level, a mutable new level and a cancel that also puts saturation back. No Fabric event fires at that moment or carries the mutable level. No key. |
| `hanging_break` | **Gap:** `AttackEntityCallback.EVENT` (different point and scope) | [Attack entity API][attackentity-api] lines 31–41 and [the player mixin][player-mixin] lines 34–37 fire it at `Player.attack` HEAD for every player attack on any entity, before damage, carrying the entity object. Horizon's `hanging_break_cancelled` fires where `BlockAttachedEntity.hurtServer` and its survival check would kill the frame, also for a blast or a missing wall (no player), with the entity kind and position and a cancel. Point, scope and arguments differ. No key. |
| `hanging_place` | **Gap:** none; nearest `UseBlockCallback.EVENT` (earlier interaction) | [Use block API][use-block-api] lines 29–55 and [the server game-mode mixin][interaction-server] lines 85–93 fire at `useItemOn` HEAD with the player, level, hand and block hit result; `PASS` continues and other results bypass vanilla. Horizon's `hanging_place_cancelled` fires once the frame, painting or knot is known to fit, before it is placed, with the uuid, name, kind, position and a cancel that keeps the item in hand. Point, side coverage and arguments differ; Fabric has no event at the placement decision. No key. |
| `player_changed_dimension` | **Gap:** `ServerEntityLevelChangeEvents.AFTER_PLAYER_CHANGE_LEVEL` (objects instead of ids) | [Level change API][level-change-api] line 59 declares it as `(ServerPlayer player, ServerLevel origin, ServerLevel destination)`, fired after the move by [the player-list mixin][sp-mixin] lines 36–42 at `respawn` TAIL (when levels differ) and [the server-player mixin][sp-events-mixin] lines 84–86 at `triggerDimensionChangeTriggers` TAIL. Both are server notifications after movement, like Horizon's `tell_mods_changed_dimension` (portals, cross-level teleports and a death respawn). Horizon passes the uuid, name and the two dimension ids only, not Fabric's player and level objects. IDs instead of objects. No key. |
| `player_clone` | **Gap:** `ServerPlayerEvents.COPY_FROM` (full player objects) | [Server player events][sp-api] line 34 declares `(ServerPlayer oldPlayer, ServerPlayer newPlayer, boolean alive)`, fired by [the server player mixin][sp-events-mixin] lines 89–91 at `ServerPlayer.restoreFrom` TAIL, after the data copy and before placement — the moment `guest_respawn` and `handle_server_portal` fire the hook. Horizon supplies the replacement UUID, name, `was_death` (`alive` inverted), and a hook-scoped `OriginalPlayerRef` exposing all original attachments, inventory, position, dimension and level, on native and wasm. The original is a captured view, not Fabric's full mutable `ServerPlayer` object; that remaining contract difference still prevents a counterpart key. |

The change is docs only: no keys, dispatch, tick-time work, adapter, alias or gameplay change.

## Event counterparts: base player hooks (#1014)

Fabric evidence is at `fabric-26.3/` pinned `2065040b236a0d5ac011bd6a45625dc49579b222`. Horizon call sites are named
by function. Scope: the twelve `base` hooks `player_destroy_item`, `player_fish`, `player_flower_pot_manipulate`,
`player_game_mode_changed`, `player_insert_lectern_book`, `player_item_frame_change`, `player_item_mend`,
`player_lectern_page_change`, `player_sprint`, `player_swap_hands`, `player_take_lectern_book` and `player_teleported`;
all twelve record a gap and no keys are added.

| Horizon hook | Fabric event considered / status | Evidence and porting boundary |
|---|---|---|
| `player_destroy_item` | **Gap:** none; nearest `PlayerBlockBreakEvents.AFTER` (different subject) | [Block break API][pbb-api] lines 58-64 and [the server game-mode mixin][spgm-mixin] lines 114-122 fire after a *block* is destroyed, with the level, player, position, old state and block entity. Horizon's `Game::native_player_destroy_item` fires from `player_equipment_broke` at the item-break notification, passing the player uuid, name, item id and slot name for a broken item; Fabric API 26.3 has no item durability or break event. No key. |
| `player_fish` | **Gap:** none; nearest `LootTableEvents.MODIFY_DROPS` (later, different arguments) | [Loot table API][lt-api] line 156 and [the loot table mixin][lt-table-mixin] lines 49-57 expose the table holder, context and mutable drops when loot is rolled. Horizon's `use_fishing_rod` and `catching_fish` fire for the cast, bite and reel-in with a state and a cancel before the bobber acts; the loot event is later, server-side, and carries no cast/bite state or cancel. No key. |
| `player_flower_pot_manipulate` | **Gap:** none; nearest `UseBlockCallback.EVENT` (before the pot is filled) | [Use block API][use-block-api] lines 29-52 and [the server game-mode mixin][interaction-server] lines 85-93 fire at `useItemOn` HEAD with the player, level, hand and hit result, before vanilla runs `FlowerPotBlock`. Horizon's `use_hand` fires after the potted state is stored and the item consumed, with the position and plant id. Moment and arguments differ; Fabric also fires on the client and can bypass vanilla, while this server notification cannot cancel. No key. |
| `player_game_mode_changed` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no game-mode change event; the nearest candidates, the creative-tab output and message events, only edit tab contents or chat and never see `ServerPlayer.setGameMode`. Horizon's `set_game_mode` and `guest_respawn` fire before the mode changes with the player uuid, name, the from mode, a target mode a listener may redirect, and a cancel. No key. |
| `player_insert_lectern_book` | **Gap:** none; nearest `UseBlockCallback.EVENT` (before the block interaction) | [Use block API][use-block-api] lines 29-52 and [the server game-mode mixin][interaction-server] lines 85-93 fire at `useItemOn` HEAD with the player, level, hand and hit result, before the block interaction; `PASS` continues and other results bypass vanilla. Horizon's `use_lectern` fires after the lectern block entity and inventory changed, with the position and book id; Fabric has no lectern event. No key. |
| `player_item_frame_change` | **Gap:** none; nearest `UseEntityCallback.EVENT` and `AttackEntityCallback.EVENT` (before interaction) | [Use entity API][use-entity-api] lines 29-62 and [its server packet mixin][use-entity-server-mixin] lines 83-92 fire before an entity interaction; [Attack entity API][attackentity-api] lines 31-41 and [the player mixin][player-mixin] lines 34-37 fire at `Player.attack` HEAD. Horizon's `interact_frame` and `attack_frame` fire after the frame content or rotation changed, with the entity id, action, item id and rotation. Point and arguments differ; Fabric also fires on the client and accepts a result (`PASS` continues), while this server notification cannot cancel. No key. |
| `player_item_mend` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no durability, repair or mending event and the pinned index lists nothing for `ItemStack` repair. Horizon's `mending` fires after the experience orb repairs the item, with the player uuid, name, item id, repaired durability and orb value. No Fabric event fires at that moment or carries those amounts. No key. |
| `player_lectern_page_change` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no lectern or menu-data event; the pinned index lists nothing for `LecternMenu` or `LecternBlockEntity`. Horizon's `lectern_menu_done` fires after the page and redstone pulse changed, with the position and new page. No Fabric event fires at that moment. No key. |
| `player_sprint` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 has no sprint start/stop event; its player events are interaction callbacks and message events, none at `ServerGamePacketListenerImpl.handlePlayerCommand`. Horizon's `lan_guest_packet` fires on `START_SPRINTING`/`STOP_SPRINTING` after the hub has set the sprint, with the player uuid, name and the new sprinting flag. No key. |
| `player_swap_hands` | **Gap:** none; nearest `UseItemCallback.EVENT` (different action) | [Use item API][use-item-api] lines 28-51 and [the server game-mode mixin][interaction-server] lines 96-103 fire for `useItem`, not for `SWAP_ITEM_WITH_OFFHAND`, and there is no hand-swap event in Fabric API 26.3. Horizon's `lan_guest_packet` fires before the main and off hand swap, with a cancel that restores the client's slots. No key. |
| `player_take_lectern_book` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no lectern event; `UseItemCallback` only fires when an item is used, not when the lectern button takes the book. Horizon's `take_lectern_book` fires after the menu and block entity changed, with the position and removed book id. No Fabric event fires there. No key. |
| `player_teleported` | **Gap:** `ServerEntityLevelChangeEvents.AFTER_PLAYER_CHANGE_LEVEL` (different trigger, objects instead of ids) | [Level change API][level-change-api] line 59 declares it as `(ServerPlayer player, ServerLevel origin, ServerLevel destination)`, fired at `respawn` TAIL when levels differ by [the player-list mixin][sp-mixin] lines 36-42 and at `triggerDimensionChangeTriggers` TAIL by [the server-player mixin][sp-events-mixin] lines 84-86. It covers cross-level moves (including cross-level respawns), not a `/tp` inside one level, and passes player and level objects. Horizon's `flush_teleport_notes` and `run_level_teleports` fire after any command teleport with the uuid, name, from/to coordinates and dimension id. No key. |
## Event counterparts: base player hooks, batch 3 (#1014)

Fabric evidence is at `fabric-26.3/` pinned `2065040b236a0d5ac011bd6a45625dc49579b222`; Horizon call sites are named by function, never by line. Scope: the twelve `base` hooks `player_totem`, `resource_pack_status`, `spawn_change`, `tnt_primed`, `vehicle_create`, `vehicle_damage`, `vehicle_destroy`, `vehicle_moved`, `world_border_bounds_changed`, `world_border_center_changed`, `player_advancement_done` and `player_advancement_progress`. All twelve record a gap and no keys are added.

| Horizon hook | Fabric event considered / status | Evidence and porting boundary |
|---|---|---|
| `player_totem` | **Gap:** none; nearest `ServerLivingEntityEvents.ALLOW_DEATH` | [Living entity API][living-api] line 75 declares `ALLOW_DEATH`, invoked from [the living entity mixin][living-mixin] line 77 through a redirect on `isDeadOrDying()` inside `hurtServer`; it only vetoes a death already in progress and passes the entity, source and amount. Horizon's `apply_totem` fires only when `totem_used` says a totem actually held the save, after the health state machine decided it, with a server player's uuid and name and no cancel. No Fabric event marks a totem save or names the totem, so moment and arguments differ. No key. |
| `resource_pack_status` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no event for `ServerboundResourcePackPacket` or a client's pack download status; the nearest lifecycle events fire for the server reloading its own data packs ([Server lifecycle API][sl-api] lines 90-104, [server mixin][server-mixin] lines 96-123) and never see a client's answer. Horizon's `lan_guest_packet` handles `S::ResourcePack` after the packet arrived, swapping the guest in and passing their uuid, name and the raw action number. No Fabric event fires there. No key. |
| `spawn_change` | **Gap:** none; nearest `EntitySleepEvents.ALLOW_SETTING_SPAWN` | [Sleep API][sleep-api] lines 170-175 and [the server player mixin][base-block-server-player-mixin] lines 102-110 fire when a sleeping player sets *their own* respawn position, with a player and a bed position, and can veto it. Horizon's `set_world_spawn` replaces the level's world spawn point and angle and only listens. Different subject and different arguments. No key. |
| `tnt_primed` | **Gap:** none; nearest `ServerEntityEvents.ALLOW_LOAD` (entity admission, different arguments) | [Entity API][sentity-api] lines 49-60 exposes a veto before any entity loads, with the entity, level, spawn reason and disk-load flag; invoked at `addEntity` HEAD by [the entity manager mixin][base-player-3-entity-manager] lines 34-41. This is not a TNT block/item priming callback. Horizon's `tnt_prime_allowed` fires with the acting player's uuid (absent for fire, redstone, an arrow or a dispenser), the block position and a cancel that preserves or restores the block or dispenser item. Entity admission does not prove that priming-side-effect rollback, and the arguments differ. No key. |
| `vehicle_create` | **Gap:** `ServerEntityEvents.ENTITY_LOAD` (broader trigger, entity object instead of kind and position) | [Entity API][sentity-api] lines 36-47 fires for any entity loaded into the server level, including disk loads, with the entity and level; [the entity callbacks mixin][sentity-callbacks-mixin] lines 38-40 invokes it at `onTrackingStart` TAIL. Horizon's `place_boat` and `place_minecart` fire after a newly placed vehicle was pushed into the level, with the vehicle type id and its position. Creation-only scope and listener arguments differ. No key. |
| `vehicle_damage` | **Gap:** none; nearest `ServerLivingEntityEvents.ALLOW_DAMAGE` and `AFTER_DAMAGE` | [Living entity API][living-api] lines 31-56 declare both, fired from `hurtServer` by [the living entity mixin][living-mixin] lines 76-92; boats and minecarts are not living entities, so neither fires for them. `ALLOW_DAMAGE` only vetoes, while Horizon can also rewrite the amount. Horizon's `hurt_boat_result` and `hurt_cart_result` fire before the wobble and damage are applied, with the vehicle kind, a mutable damage and a cancel that leaves both unchanged. No key. |
| `vehicle_destroy` | **Gap:** `ServerEntityEvents.ENTITY_UNLOAD` (broader trigger, different arguments) | [Entity API][sentity-api] lines 62-71 exposes the entity and level before unloading, and [the entity callbacks mixin][sentity-callbacks-mixin] lines 43-45 invokes it at `onTrackingEnd` HEAD for removals beyond vehicle destruction. Horizon's `hurt_boat_result` and `hurt_cart_result` fire before the vehicle is removed, with its kind and position, skipping a TNT minecart that primes instead; the boat fires before container release, while the cart fires after it. Scope, arguments and the container-release boundary differ. No key. |
| `vehicle_moved` | **Gap:** none; nearest `ServerTickEvents.END_LEVEL_TICK` | [Tick API][st-api] lines 69-81 exposes only the level at tick end, invoked at `tick` TAIL by [the level mixin][server-level-mixin] lines 39-41. Horizon's `tick_vehicle_watch` compares each watched vehicle's position once a tick and fires only on movement with the rider's uuid (or none), the vehicle kind and both positions. Fabric has no corresponding movement notification or vehicle arguments. No key. |
| `world_border_bounds_changed` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no world border event, and nothing fires at `WorldBorder.setSize` or `lerpSizeBetween`; the level lifecycle and tick events never see either call. Horizon's `cmd_worldborder` fires after the border size or its timed change was applied, listen-only. No key. |
| `world_border_center_changed` | **Gap:** none | As above, Fabric API 26.3 has no world border event and nothing fires at `WorldBorder.setCenter`. Horizon's `cmd_worldborder` fires after the center was stored, listen-only. No key. |
| `player_advancement_done` | **Gap:** none; nearest `AdvancementEvents` (load time) | [Advancement API][adv-api] lines 90-93 declare `ALL_LOADED`, fired after a registry reload by [the reload mixin][data-adv-load] lines 41-49, while `REPLACE` and `MODIFY` rewrite definitions as they load, at definition load/reload rather than player progress mutation. Horizon's `award_definition` fires when a player's progress recorded a completion, with the player uuid, name and advancement id. Fabric API 26.3 has no advancement completion event. No key. |
| `player_advancement_progress` | **Gap:** none | The same load-time `AdvancementEvents` never see a criterion granted or revoked, and Fabric API 26.3 declares no progress or criteria event. Horizon's `advancement_progress` and `run_native_advancement_grants` fire after one criterion changed and before any rewards, with the player uuid and entity id, the advancement and criterion, grant or revoke and an owned snapshot of every criterion. No Fabric event fires at that mutation or carries it. No key. |

[base-player-3-entity-manager]: https://github.com/FabricMC/fabric/blob/2065040b236a0d5ac011bd6a45625dc49579b222/fabric-lifecycle-events-v1/src/main/java/net/fabricmc/fabric/mixin/event/lifecycle/PersistentEntitySectionManagerMixin.java
## Event counterparts: base player hooks, batch 4 (#1014)

Fabric evidence is at `fabric-26.3/` pinned `2065040b236a0d5ac011bd6a45625dc49579b222`; Horizon call sites are named by function, never by line. Scope: the twelve `base` hooks `player_arm_swing`, `player_attack_cooldown_reset`, `player_bed_enter`, `player_bed_leave`, `player_bucket_entity`, `player_bundle_item_select`, `player_change_beacon_effect`, `player_deep_sleep`, `player_edit_book`, `player_egg_throw`, `player_elytra_boost` and `player_exp_cooldown_change`. All twelve record a gap and no keys are added.

| Horizon hook | Fabric event considered / status | Evidence and porting boundary |
|---|---|---|
| `player_arm_swing` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no player arm-swing event, and none of the interaction or entity events fires from `ServerGamePacketListenerImpl.handlePunch` or from the swing broadcast. Horizon's `lan_guest_packet` fires after it applied the guest's main-hand swing and reset their attack strength, passing the uuid, the name and a fixed main hand. No Fabric event fires at that moment or carries a swing. No key. |
| `player_attack_cooldown_reset` | **Gap:** none; nearest `AttackEntityCallback.EVENT` | [Attack entity API][attackentity-api] lines 31-51 declares it, and it is invoked at `Player.attack` HEAD by [the interaction player mixin][player-mixin] lines 34-40 and at the client `MultiPlayerGameMode.attack` send by [the client game mode mixin][interaction-client] lines 133-140. Both run before the attack is applied, carry the target entity and hit result, and return an `InteractionResult` that cancels. Horizon fires after the server already applied the swing and the reset with only the uuid and name, listen-only, so the moment and arguments differ. No key. |
| `player_bed_enter` | **Gap:** `EntitySleepEvents.ALLOW_SLEEPING` | [Sleep API][sleep-api] lines 53-78 and 217-226 describe an additional check after vanilla sleep checks, returning a nullable `BedSleepingProblem`; [the sleep player mixin][sleep-player-mixin] lines 36-42 injects at `Player.startSleepInBed` HEAD. HEAD here does not prove that the server sleep guards have not run. Horizon's `try_sleep` fires after its checks, passing uuid, name and bed coordinates with only a boolean veto; Fabric passes a player object and position and can select a failure reason. Arguments and cancellation output differ. No key. |
| `player_bed_leave` | **Gap:** `EntitySleepEvents.STOP_SLEEPING` | [Sleep API][sleep-api] lines 89-96 and 240-248 declares a wake-up notification with a living entity object and bed position; [living mixin][living-mixin] lines 101-108 fires at `stopSleeping` HEAD only when a sleeping position exists. Horizon's `wake_up` fires after changing sleeping state; `wake_guest_from` fires before its wake-pose and motion updates. Horizon supplies uuid, name and bed coordinates rather than an entity object, is server-only, and has no single matching firing point across these paths. No key. |
| `player_bucket_entity` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no event for catching a mob in a bucket, and no item-use or entity event fires after the mob is removed and the filled bucket is given. Horizon's `interact_mob` fires at exactly that point, with the uuid, name, entity id and species id. No key. |
| `player_bundle_item_select` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no event for `ServerboundBundleItemSelectedPacket` or for a bundle's selected index changing; the container and screen events are client menu hooks. Horizon's `lan_guest_packet` fires after `select_bundle_item` stored the selection, with the uuid, name, slot and index. No key. |
| `player_change_beacon_effect` | **Gap:** none; nearest `ServerMobEffectEvents.AFTER_ADD` and `AFTER_REMOVE` | [Effect API][effect-api] lines 79-90, 136-147, 190-200 and 230-240 declares both, invoked after an effect instance is added to or removed from a living entity by [the effect mixin][effect-mixin] lines 95-104 and 209-221; they carry a mob effect instance and the entity. A beacon menu selection is a block-entity field change, not an effect application. Horizon's `lan_station_beacon` fires after the beacon stored its primary and secondary option, with the uuid, name, position and both `EffectKey`s. Subject and arguments differ. No key. |
| `player_deep_sleep` | **Gap:** `EntitySleepEvents.ALLOW_RESETTING_TIME` | [Sleep API][sleep-api] lines 139-153 and 300-308 provides a boolean veto over whether a player counts for skipping the night; [sleep player mixin][sleep-player-mixin] lines 45-49 invokes it whenever `isSleepingLongEnough` returns true. Horizon's `guest_sleep_tick` records the transition across 100 ticks and later emits a listen-only notice once for that transition, passing uuid and name. Frequency, cancellation and player arguments differ. No key. |
| `player_edit_book` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no event for `ServerboundEditBookPacket`, and nothing fires before a book edit or signature is applied. Horizon's `lan_edit_book` fires after the slot and title filter checks passed, with the uuid, name, page count, whether a title is present and a cancel that drops the packet. No key. |
| `player_egg_throw` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no event for a thrown egg or for a projectile launched from item use, and no event fires at the spawn of the egg entity. Horizon's `throw_item` fires after the projectile was launched, the sound played and the held stack decremented, with the uuid and name. No key. |
| `player_elytra_boost` | **Gap:** none; nearest `EntityElytraEvents.ALLOW` and `CUSTOM` | [Elytra API][elytra-api] lines 28-57 declares both, invoked by [the elytra mixin][elytra-mixin] lines 45-76 from `updateFallFlying` and `canGlide` to block or replace elytra flight for an entity. They do not fire when a firework rocket is used. Horizon's `boost_firework` fires after the rocket entity was spawned, the held rocket consumed and the stat awarded, with the uuid and name. Firing point and arguments differ. No key. |
| `player_exp_cooldown_change` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no experience-orb pickup or pickup-cooldown event, and the tick and entity lifecycle events do not supply a pickup-cooldown change or its new value. Horizon's `guest_orbs` fires after the guest's experience delay was decremented, with the uuid, name and the new tick count. No key. |

[sleep-player-mixin]: https://github.com/FabricMC/fabric/blob/2065040b236a0d5ac011bd6a45625dc49579b222/fabric-entity-events-v1/src/main/java/net/fabricmc/fabric/mixin/entity/event/PlayerMixin.java
## Event counterparts: base player hooks, batch 5 (#1014)

Fabric evidence is at `fabric-26.3/` pinned `2065040b236a0d5ac011bd6a45625dc49579b222`; Horizon call sites are named by function, never by line. Scope: the twelve `base` hooks `player_input`, `player_item_cooldown`, `player_switch_hotbar_slot_pre`, `player_switch_hotbar_slot_post`, `player_jump`, `player_launch_projectile`, `player_level_change`, `player_loom_pattern_select`, `player_map_filled`, `player_name_entity`, `player_open_sign` and `player_pick_item`. All twelve record a gap and no keys are added.

| Horizon hook | Fabric event considered / status | Evidence and porting boundary |
|---|---|---|
| `player_input` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no player input or input-flags event, and no connection or player event carries the vanilla `PlayerInput` flags. Horizon's `lan_process_events` fires after the guest's flags were already applied to the hub, with the uuid, the name and the raw flag bits, listen-only. No Fabric event fires at that moment or supplies the flags. No key. |
| `player_item_cooldown` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no item-cooldown event; the dossier records only NeoForge and Paper counterparts. Horizon's `apply_use_cooldown` fires before the group cooldown is started, with the uuid, name, item id, ticks and a boolean veto, and `blocked_by_attacker` fires the same hook for a shield disable. No Fabric event covers either moment. No key. |
| `player_switch_hotbar_slot_pre` | **Gap:** `ClientHotbarScrollEvents.ALLOW` / `BEFORE` | [The hotbar scroll API][hotbar-scroll-api] lines 28-51 declares both, and [the mouse handler mixin][hotbar-scroll-mixin] lines 32-52 invokes them on the client from `MouseHandler.onScroll` around `Inventory.setSelectedSlot`, passing an `Inventory` and scroll offsets. Horizon's `lan_guest_packet` fires on the server for the guest's set-carried-item packet after the range check and only when the slot differs, with the uuid, entity id, old and new slot and a veto. Side, firing point and arguments differ. No key. |
| `player_switch_hotbar_slot_post` | **Gap:** `ClientHotbarScrollEvents.AFTER` | [The hotbar scroll API][hotbar-scroll-api] lines 57-62 and [the mouse handler mixin][hotbar-scroll-mixin] lines 44-52 fire on the client immediately after a scroll changed the selected slot, with the inventory and offsets. Horizon's `lan_guest_packet` fires on the server after the guest's selected slot was stored and any main-hand use stopped, with the uuid, entity id and both slots. Side, firing point and arguments differ. No key. |
| `player_passage_cross` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no portal or passage event; Immersive Portals' own check (`Portal.canTeleportEntity`) is a mod's. Horizon's `guest_passage_cross` fires the hook on the server for a client's crossing within a level, after the move to its source passed and before the server computes the destination, with the player, the passage, the feet before and a veto. No key. |
| `player_jump` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no jump event, and no entity or movement event fires when an accepted server movement makes the guest jump from the ground. Horizon's `guest_jump` fires after the movement succeeded and the jump was applied, with the uuid and name, listen-only. No key. |
| `player_launch_projectile` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no projectile-launch event, and the interaction events run before item use starts rather than after the projectile entity exists. Horizon's `leave_guest` fires after the guest's projectile was launched, with the uuid, the name and the projectile's held item id. No key. |
| `player_level_change` | **Gap:** none; nearest `ServerEntityLevelChangeEvents` | [The level-change API][level-change-api] lines 32-62 declares the dimension-change notifications `AFTER_ENTITY_CHANGE_LEVEL` and `AFTER_PLAYER_CHANGE_LEVEL`, which report movement between levels with entity/player objects and origin/destination levels, including a player respawn into a different level. Horizon's `native_player_level_changed` fires after an experience level change, with the uuid, name and both levels. Subject and arguments differ. No key. |
| `player_loom_pattern_select` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no loom or server menu-button event; the container and screen events are client menu hooks. Horizon's `lan_station_button` fires after the loom menu accepted the button, with the uuid, name and the zero-based pattern index. No key. |
| `player_map_filled` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no empty-map-use or map-filled event, and no item event fires after the filled map and its saved data were created. Horizon's `use_empty_map` fires after the fill, the stack swap and the stat award, with the uuid, name and the new map id. No key. |
| `player_name_entity` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no name-tag or entity-rename event. Horizon's `name_mob` and `interact_armor_stand` fire after the custom name and persistence were set and the tag was consumed, with the uuid, name, entity id and new name. No Fabric event fires at that moment or carries the rename. No key. |
| `player_open_sign` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no event for a server player opening a sign editor, and no block or menu event fires when the server builds the editor state. Horizon's `open_sign_editor` fires after the editor state was built, with the uuid, name, position and front flag. No key. |
| `player_pick_item` | **Gap:** `PlayerPickItemEvents.BLOCK` and `.ENTITY` | [The pick-item API][base-player-pick-api] lines 36-66 and 69-93 declares the server callbacks and nullable stack result; [the server packet mixin][use-entity-server-mixin] lines 55-80 invokes the two events at `BlockState.getCloneItemStack` and `Entity.getPickResult` inside `handlePickItemFromBlock` and `handlePickItemFromEntity`, passing the player alongside the position and state or the entity, and replacing the picked stack. Horizon's `guest_pick_item` uses one hook for both cases after the stack was resolved, with the uuid, name, a `from_entity` flag, integer position and entity id and a veto. Split events, object-versus-id arguments and replace-versus-veto control differ. No key. |

[base-player-pick-api]: https://github.com/FabricMC/fabric/blob/2065040b236a0d5ac011bd6a45625dc49579b222/fabric-events-interaction-v0/src/main/java/net/fabricmc/fabric/api/event/player/PlayerPickItemEvents.java
## Event counterparts: base player hooks, batch 6 (#1014)

Fabric evidence is at `fabric-26.3/` pinned `2065040b236a0d5ac011bd6a45625dc49579b222`; Horizon call sites are named by function, never by line. Scope: the eleven `base` hooks `player_pickup_arrow`, `player_ready_arrow`, `player_recipe_book_click`, `player_recipe_book_settings_change`, `player_recipe_discovered`, `player_riptide`, `player_shear_entity`, `player_shield_disable`, `player_stat_award`, `player_stonecutter_recipe_select` and `player_stop_spectating_entity`, with `player_start_spectating_entity` beside its mirror. All twelve record a gap and no keys are added.

| Horizon hook | Fabric event considered / status | Evidence and porting boundary |
|---|---|---|
| `player_pickup_arrow` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no arrow-pickup or projectile-pickup event, and the item, interaction and player events fire at use, attack or block-break points rather than after a projectile was removed and its stack taken. Horizon's `native_player_pickup_arrow`, called from `guest_arrows`, fires after the pickup was applied, with the uuid, name, entity id and pickup item id, listen-only. No key. |
| `player_ready_arrow` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no bow-release event; the use-item callback runs where item use begins, before the draw is released, and carries no selected projectile. Horizon's `release_using_item` fires after the bow stack and its projectile slot were resolved and the hand check passed, with the uuid, name and the arrow item id, listen-only. No key. |
| `player_recipe_book_click` | **Gap:** none; nearest `ClientRecipeSynchronizedEvent.EVENT` | [The recipe-sync API][recipe-sync-api] lines 25-39 and [its implementation][recipe-sync-impl] lines 54-55 describe a client-side event fired when the client receives all synchronized recipes from a server supporting Fabric recipe sync, carrying the client and the recipe set. Horizon's `lan_place_recipe` fires on the server after the recipe id was resolved and found known and placeable, before the grid placement, with the uuid, name, recipe id and the shift-click flag, listen-only. Side, moment and arguments differ; `ItemClickBehaviorCallback.EVENT` instead overrides stack-on-slot clicks with stack/slot objects and `EventResult` control ([the item-click API][item-click-api] lines 28-56; [the menu mixin][item-click-mixin] lines 37-49), not recipe placement. No key. |
| `player_recipe_book_settings_change` | **Gap:** none | The only recipe event in 26.3 is `ClientRecipeSynchronizedEvent.EVENT` ([the recipe-sync API][recipe-sync-api] lines 25-39), which reports a client's recipe set, not a settings packet. Horizon's `lan_process_events` fires on the server when the guest's recipe-book settings packet arrives and its raw ids were decoded, with the uuid, name, book protocol id, open and filtering flags, listen-only. Side and moment differ. No key. |
| `player_recipe_discovered` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no recipe-unlock event; `ClientRecipeSynchronizedEvent.EVENT` ([the recipe-sync API][recipe-sync-api] lines 25-39) only reports recipes a client already received. Horizon's `recipe_unlocked` fires per recipe after `ServerRecipeBook.addRecipes`, before toasts and advancement triggers, with the uuid, name, recipe id and a veto implemented by removing refused recipes from the book. Side, moment, arguments and cancellation differ. No key. |
| `player_riptide` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no riptide or trident-launch event. Horizon's `release_trident` fires on the server after the velocity, spin attack and grounded lift were applied, with the uuid and name, listen-only. No key. |
| `player_shear_entity` | **Gap:** nearest `UseEntityCallback.EVENT` | Fabric API 26.3 declares no shear event. [The interaction API][use-entity-api] lines 28-65 supplies player/entity objects, hand and hit result with `InteractionResult` control (`PASS` continues); [the server packet mixin][use-entity-server-mixin] lines 83-92 fires before item interaction and the mob's shearable guards. Horizon's `interact_mob` fires after the held-item and shearable checks, with the uuid, name, entity id, off-hand flag and a veto. Firing point, object-versus-id arguments and result-versus-veto control differ. No key. |
| `player_shield_disable` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no shield-disable event, and the attack events fire before damage and blocking are resolved. Horizon's `blocked_by_attacker` fires after a fully or partly blocked hit, with the uuid, name and the cooldown ticks, before the item cooldown and stop-using are applied, listen-only. No key. |
| `player_stat_award` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no statistic-award event; no player or lifecycle event fires before a stat count and its scoreboard criteria change. Horizon's `award_stat` and `award_leave_game` fire before the count is added, with the uuid, name, stat type and stat ids, the value and a veto. No key. |
| `player_stonecutter_recipe_select` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no server menu-button event; the recipe event `ClientRecipeSynchronizedEvent.EVENT` ([the recipe-sync API][recipe-sync-api] lines 25-39) is client recipe sync. Horizon's `lan_station_button` fires after the station menu accepted the button, with the uuid, name and the zero-based recipe index, listen-only. No key. |
| `player_stop_spectating_entity` | **Gap:** nearest `EntityTrackingEvents.STOP_TRACKING` | [The tracking API][tracking-api] lines 41-49 and 63-70 declares `STOP_TRACKING`, invoked at `removePairing` HEAD in [the server entity mixin][tracking-mixin] lines 43-46 when a player stops tracking an entity; it fires for every tracking removal, including chunk unloads, carries the entity and player objects, and is not the spectate camera. Horizon's `guest_set_camera` fires when the guest's camera returns to their own entity, with the uuid, name and the previously watched entity id, listen-only. Moment, guards and arguments differ. No key. |
| `player_start_spectating_entity` | **Gap:** nearest `EntityTrackingEvents.START_TRACKING` | [The tracking API][tracking-api] lines 30-39 declares `START_TRACKING`, invoked in [the server entity mixin][tracking-mixin] lines 38-41 after a player started tracking an entity and its spawn packet was sent; it fires for every new pairing, carries the entity and player objects, and is not the spectate camera. Horizon's `guest_set_camera` fires after the guest's camera moved to another entity and the client was told, with the uuid, name and the watched entity id, listen-only. Moment, guards and arguments differ. No key. |

## Event counterparts: base player hooks, batch 7 (#1014)

Fabric evidence is at `fabric-26.3/` pinned `2065040b236a0d5ac011bd6a45625dc49579b222`; Horizon call sites are named by function, never by line. Scope: the twelve `base` hooks `player_stop_using_item`, `player_swap_with_equipment_slot`, `player_teleport_end_gateway`, `player_toggle_entity_age_lock`, `player_toggle_sneak`, `player_trade`, `player_unleash_entity`, `player_velocity`, `portal_create`, `potion_splash`, `prepare_hopper_transfer` and `prepare_item_craft`. All twelve record a gap and no keys are added.

| Horizon hook | Fabric event considered / status | Evidence and porting boundary |
|---|---|---|
| `player_stop_using_item` | **Gap:** none; unrelated `EntitySleepEvents.STOP_SLEEPING` | [The sleep API][sleep-api] lines 89-92 and 241-248 declare `STOP_SLEEPING`, fired at `LivingEntity.stopSleeping` HEAD in [the entity-event mixin][sliving-mixin] lines 101-109 whenever a sleeping entity wakes, carrying the entity and its sleeping position; [the tracking API][tracking-api] lines 41-45 fires `STOP_TRACKING` when a player stops tracking an entity. Neither is item use. Horizon's `release_using_item` fires after the used hand, stack and tick count were resolved and the item released, with the uuid and name only, listen-only. Moment, arguments and per-entity scope differ. No key. |
| `player_swap_with_equipment_slot` | **Gap:** nearest `ServerEntityEvents.EQUIPMENT_CHANGE` | [The server entity API][sentity-api] lines 73-79 and 106-108 declare `EQUIPMENT_CHANGE` with the living entity, the `EquipmentSlot` and both stacks; [the lifecycle mixin][equipment-mixin] lines 32-40 fires it inside `collectEquipmentChanges` at the `Map.put` call, during `LivingEntity.tick`, once per changed slot and only when `ItemStack.matches` reports a change. Horizon's `lan_guest_packet` fires once, immediately in the guest swap handler after both inventory slots and the equip callbacks changed, with the uuid, name and the single slot `offhand`, listen-only. Firing point, per-slot splitting and object-versus-id/stack arguments differ. No key. |
| `player_teleport_end_gateway` | **Gap:** none; nearest `ServerEntityLevelChangeEvents.AFTER_PLAYER_CHANGE_LEVEL` | The level-change API ([the level-change API][level-change-api] lines 48-63) reports a completed change-level, with the player and both levels but no gateway position or veto. It fires at `respawn` TAIL when levels differ ([the player-list mixin][base-player-6-list-mixin] lines 36-43) and at `triggerDimensionChangeTriggers` TAIL ([the server-player mixin][base-block-server-player-mixin] lines 84-87). Fabric API 26.3 declares no End gateway or teleport event. Horizon's `handle_server_portal` fires before the gateway is entered and its cooldown starts, with the uuid, name, gateway block position and a cancel flag. Moment, arguments and control differ. No key. |
| `player_toggle_entity_age_lock` | **Gap:** none; nearest `UseEntityCallback.EVENT` | Fabric API 26.3 declares no age-lock event. [The interaction API][use-entity-api] lines 28-65 supplies player/entity objects, hand and hit result with `InteractionResult` control (`PASS` continues); [the server packet mixin][use-entity-server-mixin] lines 83-92 fires before item interaction and the mob's own `mobInteract` guards; Horizon's `interact_mob` fires after `age_lock_takes` and `set_age_locked` applied the change, with the uuid, name, entity id and the new locked flag, listen-only. Moment and arguments differ. No key. |
| `player_toggle_sneak` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no sneak or player-input-state event; the closest player events are connection and level-change notifications. Horizon's `lan_process_events` fires on the server when the guest's player-input packet reported a new sneak state and the hub applied it, with the uuid, name and the sneaking flag, listen-only. No key. |
| `player_trade` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no merchant or trade event, and the entity lifecycle events fire on load, equipment change or level change rather than after a trade. Horizon's `lan_merchant_op` fires after `merchant_apply` applied the guest's trades, with the uuid, name and the merchant entity id, listen-only. No key. |
| `player_unleash_entity` | **Gap:** none; nearest `EntityTrackingEvents.STOP_TRACKING` | [The tracking API][tracking-api] lines 41-45 and 63-70 declares `STOP_TRACKING`, fired at `removePairing` HEAD in [the server entity mixin][tracking-mixin] lines 43-46 when a player stops tracking an entity; that is network tracking, not a leash. Horizon's `drop_leash` fires after `leash::remove` cleared the leash and only when the acting player used the lead, with the uuid, name and entity id, listen-only. Moment and guards differ. No key. |
| `player_velocity` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no velocity, motion or entity-sync event; the entity events it has fire on load, death, equipment change, tracking and level change, not when changed motion is sent. Horizon's `guest_server_tick` fires when a guest's changed motion is about to be synced, with the uuid, name and the velocity vector, listen-only. No key. |
| `portal_create` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no portal-creation or platform-creation event; `ServerEntityLevelChangeEvents` ([the level-change API][level-change-api] lines 48-63) reports a completed dimension change with no block position. Horizon's `nether_exit_portal` and `end_arrival` fire after the portal rectangle or End platform blocks were placed, with the dimension id and the corner or origin position, listen-only. Moment and arguments differ. No key. |
| `potion_splash` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no potion-splash or projectile-impact-after-effect event; the interaction events fire at use or hit before the splash effects are applied. Horizon's `tick_thrown_potions` and `projectile_hit_landed` fire after `splash` applied the hit effects, with the potion item id and position, listen-only, and skip experience bottles. No key. |
| `prepare_hopper_transfer` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no hopper or block-entity transfer event; its only `prepare`-named event, `PermissionEvents.PREPARE_OFFLINE_PLAYER`, prepares a permission context and is unrelated. Horizon's `hopper_try_move_items` fires after the cooldown, enabled and view guards and before pushing or pulling items, with the hopper position, listen-only. No key. |
| `prepare_item_craft` | **Gap:** none; nearest `ClientRecipeSynchronizedEvent.EVENT` | [The recipe-sync API][recipe-sync-api] lines 25-39 describes a client-side event reporting the synchronized recipe set, not a crafting result, and Fabric API 26.3 declares no crafting-result or result-slot event. Horizon's `guest_craft_result` and `lan_inventory_click` call `native_prepare_item_craft` on the server with the uuid, name, the crafting-table position (`None` for the inventory grid) and a mutable result stack before it is sent, replace contract. Side, moment and arguments differ. No key. |

## Event counterparts: base prepare hooks (#1014)

Fabric evidence is at `2065040b236a0d5ac011bd6a45625dc49579b222` (`fabric-26.3/`); Horizon call sites are named by function, never by line. Scope: the twelve `base` hooks `prepare_item_enchant`, `prepare_smithing`, `projectile_launch`, `raid_spawn_wave`, `raid_state_change`, `sculk_bloom`, `sleep_finished_time`, `sponge_absorb`, `target_hit`, `thunder_change`, `time_skip` and `trade_select`. All twelve record a gap and no keys are added.

| Horizon hook | Fabric event considered / status | Evidence and porting boundary |
|---|---|---|
