| `prepare_item_enchant` | **Gap:** nearest `EnchantmentEvents.ALLOW_ENCHANTING` | [The enchantment API][enchant-events-api] line 54 declares `ALLOW_ENCHANTING`, a per-enchantment and per-item check on an `ItemStack` that can only override whether that enchantment may be applied, and line 107 declares `MODIFY_WITH_LOOKUP`, which rewrites an enchantment's definition. Neither fires when a server player's enchanting offers are calculated. `lan_enchant_offers` calls `native_prepare_item_enchant` after the three costs were computed and before they are sent, with the uuid, name, table position and a mutable three-element cost array, listen-only. Moment, arguments and control differ. No key. |
| `prepare_smithing` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no smithing, menu-result or prepare-result event; its item and block events fire at item use or block interaction (`ItemEvents`, `BlockEvents`), not when a station menu recomputes its result slot. `lan_station_op` calls `native_prepare_smithing` with the uuid, name, station position and a mutable result stack that is written back into slot 3, replace contract. No key. |
| `projectile_launch` | **Gap:** nearest `ServerEntityEvents.ENTITY_LOAD` | [The server entity API][sentity-api] lines 38-49 describes `ENTITY_LOAD` as firing for every entity loaded into a `ServerLevel`, already in the level, with the entity object and the level; `ALLOW_LOAD` at lines 52-60 only vetoes loading. Neither is projectile-specific nor reports an owner. `add_projectile` calls `native_projectile_launch` once per newly spawned projectile with the optional player uuid, name, entity id and kind string. Scope and arguments differ. No key. |
| `raid_spawn_wave` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no raid event; the generic `ServerEntityEvents.ENTITY_LOAD` ([the server entity API][sentity-api] lines 38-49) fires once per entity as it loads, not once after a whole wave and with no wave number or raider count. `spawn_wave` calls `native_raid_spawn_wave` after the wave spawn loop, with the raid id, position, one-based wave number and `spawns.len()` (the requested raider count, excluding riders and including failed spawns). Moment and arguments differ. No key. |
| `raid_state_change` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no raid or raid-state event, and no level event fires when a raid moves to `triggered`, `victory`, `loss` or `stopped`. `create_or_extend_raid` and `raid_command_stop` call `native_raid_state_change` after the stored raid changed state, with the raid reference (level and id), centre position and state string; a terminal raid removed from storage cannot be read. No key. |
| `sculk_bloom` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no sculk-catalyst, bloom or block-state-change event; the block-entity events only report load and unload. `catalyst_bloom` calls `native_sculk_bloom` after `sculk_catalyst_bloom` changed the catalyst block state, with the dimension id and block position, server-side. No key. |
| `sleep_finished_time` | **Gap:** nearest `EntitySleepEvents.ALLOW_RESETTING_TIME` | [The sleep API][sleep-api] lines 139-145 and 301 declare `ALLOW_RESETTING_TIME`, checked per sleeping player at `isSleepingLongEnough` RETURN only when vanilla returned true ([the sleep player mixin][sleep-player-mixin] lines 45-50), and able only to veto whether that player counts toward the skip. It carries the player object and no wake-up time, and it runs before the level decides to move the clock. `tick_sleep` calls `native_sleep_finished_time` once after enough players slept and `advance_time` is on, with the dimension id, current day time, a mutable next-morning time and a cancel flag, and may still cancel the jump. Moment, arguments and control differ. No key. |
| `sponge_absorb` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no sponge or fluid-absorption event; `FluidFlowEvents.ALLOW` ([the fluid flow API][fluid-flow-api] lines 30-44 and 68-74) vetoes fluid flow or a neighbor update, rather than notifying that a sponge became wet. `observe_sponge_absorb` calls `native_sponge_absorb` server-side after the sponge absorbed water and changed state, with the dimension and block position. No key. |
| `target_hit` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no target-block or projectile-hit-block event; the interaction events fire at player use or attack, before a projectile reaches a target block. `target_block_hit` calls `native_target_hit` after `target_projectile_hit` resolved the hit and computed the target's output strength, with the dimension id, block position and strength. Moment and arguments differ. No key. |
| `thunder_change` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no weather or thunder event, and the level events it has fire on load, unload or per tick without weather arguments. `cmd_weather` calls `native_thunder_change` for a `/weather` request before its cancel flags are applied, even if the requested thunder state is unchanged, with the dimension id, the new thundering flag and a cancel flag, after `native_weather_change` was already asked. No key. |
| `time_skip` | **Gap:** none; nearest `EntitySleepEvents.ALLOW_RESETTING_TIME` | [The sleep API][sleep-api] lines 139-154 and 301-308 only veto a sleeping player counting toward the skip, invoked at a successful `isSleepingLongEnough` RETURN ([the sleep player mixin][sleep-player-mixin] lines 45-50); `/time set` never passes through it. `tick_sleep` and `cmd_time` call `native_time_skip` before the clock is written, with the dimension id, the from and to times, the cause (`sleep` or `command`) and a cancel flag. Moment, arguments and scope differ. No key. |
| `trade_select` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no merchant, trade or server menu-button event; its connection and player events fire at join, respawn and leave, not at a trade selection. `lan_select_trade` calls `native_trade_select` after the merchant menu accepted the selection, with the uuid, name, villager entity id (or none) and the selected index. No key. |

[enchant-events-api]: https://github.com/FabricMC/fabric/blob/2065040b236a0d5ac011bd6a45625dc49579b222/fabric-item-api-v1/src/main/java/net/fabricmc/fabric/api/item/v1/EnchantmentEvents.java

## Event counterparts: base trade and vault hooks (#1014)

Fabric evidence is at `2065040b236a0d5ac011bd6a45625dc49579b222` (`fabric-26.3/`); Horizon call sites are named by function, never by line. Scope: the ten `base` hooks `trade_with_villager`, `vault_change_state`, `vault_loot`, `weather_change`, `whitelist_state_update`, `world_border_bounds_change_finish`, `living_drown`, `living_fall`, `living_heal` and `living_jump`. All ten record a gap and no keys are added.

| Horizon hook | Fabric event considered / status | Evidence and porting boundary |
|---|---|---|
| `trade_with_villager` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no merchant, trade or trade-offer event; its item, block, loot and player events fire at item use, block interaction, loot resolution or connection moments, not when `MerchantResultSlot.onTake` takes an offer. `merchant_traded` calls `native_trade_with_villager` once per traded index, after the statistic and the `villager_trade` trigger, with the trader entity id, kind string and the live `MerchantOffer` as a mutable `out`; the collect contract needs that offer object and nothing Fabric offers gives it. No key. |
| `vault_change_state` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no vault-state-change event; `ServerBlockEntityEvents` and `ClientBlockEntityEvents` only report block-entity load and unload, and `ServerLevelEvents` only level load and unload. `set_vault_state` calls `native_vault_change_state` after the new `vault_state` was written through `level_set`, with the dimension id, position and state name, listen-only. No key. |
| `vault_loot` | **Gap:** nearest `LootTableEvents.MODIFY_DROPS` | [The loot table API][lt-api] lines 105-110 declares `MODIFY_DROPS` and [the loot table mixin][lt-table-mixin] lines 49-57 fires it inside `getRandomItemsRaw`, once per table roll, with the table holder, `LootContext` and the whole `List<ItemStack>` of drops, for every loot table. `vault_events` calls `native_vault_loot` at `VaultEvent::Eject`, once per ejected item, with the block position and the item id string, before `spawn_item`; moment, arguments and scope differ. `ALL_LOADED` and `MODIFY` run only at data load. No key. |
| `weather_change` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no weather event; `ServerLevelEvents` reports level load and unload, and the tick events carry only the level. `cmd_weather` calls `native_weather_change` before either cancel flag is applied, with the dimension id, the requested raining and thundering flags and a cancel flag, so a veto can restore the previous weather parameters. No key. |
| `whitelist_state_update` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no whitelist, server-property or configuration-change event; `ServerLifecycleEvents` fires at start, stop, save and data-pack reload only. `set_using_whitelist` calls `native_whitelist_state_update` after the stored `white-list` property changed, with the new boolean, listen-only. No key. |
| `world_border_bounds_change_finish` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no world-border event; no level, tick or lifecycle event fires when a border's remaining time reaches zero. `tick_worldborder` calls `native_world_border_bounds_change_finish` after `border.tick()` took a running change to zero, and only when an integrated or dedicated server is present. No key. |
| `living_drown` | **Gap:** nearest `ServerLivingEntityEvents.ALLOW_DAMAGE` | [The server living API][sliving-api] lines 30-43 declares the veto-only `ALLOW_DAMAGE`, fired before `isSleeping` in `hurtServer` ([the living mixin][sliving-mixin] lines 80-85) with the entity object, damage source and amount; it has no mutable drowning flag, mutable damage or bubble count. `breathing::drown` calls `native_living_drown` when the server side has no breathable medium and air is at or below zero, with the entity id, kind, mutable `is_drowning`, mutable damage, read-only bubble count and a cancel flag, after the initial air update and before the drowning result is applied. Moment, arguments and control differ. No key. |
| `living_fall` | **Gap:** nearest `ServerLivingEntityEvents.ALLOW_DAMAGE` / `AFTER_DAMAGE` | Fabric API 26.3 declares no fall or landing event; [the server living API][sliving-api] lines 30-60 declares `ALLOW_DAMAGE` and `AFTER_DAMAGE`, fired at the `isSleeping` invocation and nonfatal TAIL of `hurtServer` respectively ([the living mixin][sliving-mixin] lines 80-94), only on the server and with the entity object and damage data rather than mutable fall distance and multiplier. `player_fall_damage` and `cause_fall_damage_at` call `native_living_fall` before fall damage and block effects, with the entity id, kind, mutable distance and multiplier and a cancel flag. Moment, side, arguments and control differ: Horizon runs on both sides and can cancel fall handling, while Fabric only vetoes damage or observes it. No key. |
| `living_heal` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no heal or health-gain event; `ServerLivingEntityEvents` covers damage, death and conversion only. `peaceful_regeneration` and `heal` call `native_living_heal` with the entity id, a mutable amount and a cancel flag, inside the tick's health update, where a deferred call would reorder vanilla's effects. No key. |
| `living_jump` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no jump event; no movement, velocity or tick event fires at a living entity's jump. `jump_from_ground` and `camel::dash` call `native_living_jump` after the velocity write, with the entity id and kind string, listen-only, with player notifications suppressed on replayed ticks. No key. |

## Event counterparts: block hooks (#1014)

Fabric evidence is at `2065040b236a0d5ac011bd6a45625dc49579b222` (`fabric-26.3/`); Horizon call sites are named by function, never by line. Scope: the four `block` hooks `block_speed_factor`, `block_grow_feature`, `block_render_visibility` and `block_tool_modification`. All four record a gap and no keys are added.

| Horizon hook | Fabric event considered / status | Evidence and porting boundary |
|---|---|---|
| `block_speed_factor` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no block speed-factor, block-property or movement-factor event; nothing fires while `Entity.getBlockSpeedFactor` reads a state's factor. `native_block_speed_factor` runs on each read, on both sides including replayed ticks, with the loop context, the state id and a mutable factor; no Fabric event carries a block state and a mutable factor at that moment. No key. |
| `block_grow_feature` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no sapling, azalea, mushroom or fungus configured-feature growth event. `block_grow_feature` calls `native_block_grow_feature` on the authoritative server with the dimension, position, a mutable feature key and a cancel flag; no Fabric event offers that growth moment or feature replacement and cancellation. No key. |
| `block_render_visibility` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no event that resolves a persistent per-state mesh-visibility table. The client rendering event `LevelRenderEvents.AFTER_SOLID_FEATURES` instead observes a frame after solid feature geometry is drawn ([the API][level-render-events] lines 77-85; [the mixin][level-renderer-mixin] lines 95-98), without a mutable hidden flag. `refresh_render_visibility` asks `native_block_render_visibility` once for every registry state on world join and explicit refresh, before remeshing with the resulting table, with the state id and a mutable hidden flag; firing point, arguments and control differ. No key. |
| `block_tool_modification` | **Gap:** nearest `UseBlockCallback.EVENT` | [The callback API][use-block-api] lines 28-54 passes the player, level, hand and hit result: `PASS` continues vanilla, while a non-`PASS` result ends processing, including success as well as failure. It fires at server `useItemOn` HEAD ([server mixin][interaction-server] lines 85-94) and before client prediction after a spectator guard ([client mixin][interaction-client] lines 98-115), before tool transformations, with no tool ability or mutable final state. `native_block_tool_modification` runs inside the tool-action path (axe strip, scrape and wax-off; shovel flatten; hoe till) and in `modified_campfire`, with the item stack, a `ToolUse` (player, hand, position, face, click point, ability, simulated) and a mutable final state plus cancel. Moment, arguments and control differ. No key. |

## Event counterparts: landing, bone meal, camera and sleep hooks (#1014)

Fabric evidence is at `2065040b236a0d5ac011bd6a45625dc49579b222` (`fabric-26.3/`); Horizon call sites are named by function, never by line. Scope: the eight hooks `fall_on`, `bounce_on`, `bonemeal`, `calculate_player_turn`, `camera_pose`, `camera_type_cycle`, `can_continue_sleeping` and `can_player_sleep`. All eight record a gap and no keys are added.

| Horizon hook | Fabric event considered / status | Evidence and porting boundary |
|---|---|---|
| `fall_on` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no block landing or fall-on event; nothing fires while `Block.fallOn` has finished computing an entity's fall damage. `native_fall_on` runs inside the entity's move with the entity object (player, mob or item), the state, position, distance, a mutable damage amount and landing write requests; no Fabric event carries that moment or those arguments. No key. |
| `bounce_on` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no bounce or restitution event; nothing fires after `Block.bounceOn` has produced an entity's bounce. `native_bounce_on` runs inside the entity's move with the entity object, the state, position, fall distance and a mutable movement vector; no Fabric event offers that moment or those arguments. No key. |
| `bonemeal` | **Gap:** nearest `UseBlockCallback.EVENT` | [The callback API][use-block-api] lines 28-54 passes the player, level, hand and hit result, with `PASS` continuing vanilla; it fires at server `useItemOn` HEAD ([server mixin][interaction-server] lines 85-94) and in client prediction ([client mixin][interaction-client] lines 98-115), before the item's use. `native_bonemeal` fires inside `BoneMealItem.growCrop` with the stack, state, position, the player or none for a dispenser/villager, the validity answer and mutable cancel and success, so moment, guards and arguments differ. No key. |
| `calculate_player_turn` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no mouse-turn or sensitivity event; its client tick events fire at tick boundaries, not while `MouseHandler.turnPlayer` reads the sensitivity and cinematic-camera options. `native_calculate_player_turn` fires for the grabbed local player with the entity id, uuid and mutable sensitivity and cinematic flag, once per look application. No key. |
| `camera_pose` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no camera setup or pose event exposing this mutable pose contract. `native_camera_pose_now` fires after `Camera.setup` with the partial tick and a mutable position, yaw and pitch, before the picture is drawn. No Fabric event offers that firing point with those mutable arguments. No key. |
| `camera_type_cycle` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no camera-type or perspective event, so nothing fires when `Minecraft.handleKeybinds` cycles `Options.setCameraType` on the F5 press. `native_camera_type_cycle` fires on the client before the type changes, with the previous type and a mutable next type that may skip or keep a type. No key. |
| `can_continue_sleeping` | **Gap:** nearest `EntitySleepEvents.ALLOW_BED`; also `START_SLEEPING` and `STOP_SLEEPING` | [The sleep API][sleep-api] lines 98-120 declares `ALLOW_BED`, fired at `checkBedExists` RETURN with the living entity, sleeping position, block state and vanilla boolean ([the living entity mixin][living-mixin] lines 111-124); it overrides bed validity, not the combined sleep problem including the player's daytime rule. `native_can_continue_sleeping` asks each tick whether a sleeping player, villager or wandering trader may stay asleep, with the uuid, name, kind, bed position, the vanilla problem and a mutable may-continue answer; arguments and decision scope differ. `START_SLEEPING` and `STOP_SLEEPING` instead notify one-shot transitions at `startSleeping` RETURN and guarded `stopSleeping` HEAD (mixin lines 94-109). No key. |
| `can_player_sleep` | **Gap:** nearest `EntitySleepEvents.ALLOW_SLEEPING` | `ALLOW_SLEEPING` injects at `startSleepInBed` HEAD and only returns a `BedSleepingProblem` veto before the vanilla problem exists ([the API][sleep-api] lines 53-68, [the player mixin][sleep-player-mixin] lines 32-40); `ALLOW_NEARBY_MONSTERS` redirects the monster-list check alone and `ALLOW_SETTING_SPAWN` wraps `setRespawnPosition` ([the server player mixin][base-block-server-player-mixin] lines 102-118). `native_can_player_sleep` fires after vanilla computed its first problem and lets a mod clear or replace it, with the uuid, name, bed position and mutable problem. Moment, arguments and control differ. No key. |
## Event counterparts: chat completion, chunk watching, raw packets and client pause hooks (#1014)

Fabric evidence is at `2065040b236a0d5ac011bd6a45625dc49579b222` (`fabric-26.3/`); Horizon call sites are named by function, never by line. Scope: the eight hooks `chat_suggestion_applied`, `chunk_watch`, `chunk_sent`, `chunk_unwatch`, `client_packet`, `client_packet_send`, `client_pause_change_pre` and `client_pause_change_post`. All eight record a gap and no keys are added.

| Horizon hook | Fabric event considered / status | Evidence and porting boundary |
|---|---|---|
| `chat_suggestion_applied` | **Gap:** none; nearest `ClientReceiveMessageEvents.ALLOW_CHAT` and `CHAT` | Those events fire as an arriving chat message is handled, before it is displayed: `ALLOW_CHAT` and `CHAT` are declared at [the receive API][receive-api] lines 39-47 and 99-108 and invoked from `ChatListenerMixin` inside `fabric_onChatMessage` ([the receive mixin][receive-mixin] lines 56-65). `native_chat_suggestion_applied` instead reports an applied completion from `notify_chat_suggestion_completion`, carrying the original input, the UTF-16 range, the suggestion and the resulting length-limited field value when Tab or a click completes the chat field. No Fabric event fires at that moment or carries those arguments. No key. |
| `chunk_watch` | **Gap:** nearest `ServerChunkEvents.CHUNK_UNLOAD` and `FULL_CHUNK_STATUS_CHANGE` | `CHUNK_UNLOAD` fires from `ChunkMap.scheduleUnload` after `setLoaded(false)`, while the chunk is still present in the level ([the chunk map mixin][chunk-map-mixin] lines 44-52); `FULL_CHUNK_STATUS_CHANGE` tracks a chunk's ticking-status transitions ([the server chunk API][server-chunk-api] lines 78-89). Neither is per player. `native_chunk_watch` runs from `run_chunk_watches` for one `ChunkWatchKind::Watch` entry after the LAN server's network step, with a `PlayerRef` and a `ChunkRef`: a player's view gaining a chunk, not the level unloading one. No key. |
| `chunk_sent` | **Gap:** none; nearest `ServerChunkEvents.CHUNK_LOAD` | Fabric API 26.3 declares no per-player chunk-send event; the server chunk events cover the level's own chunk lifecycle, load and generate ([the server chunk API][server-chunk-api] lines 43-57) and unload ([the chunk map mixin][chunk-map-mixin] lines 44-52). `native_chunk_sent` fires from `run_chunk_watches` for `ChunkWatchKind::Sent`, after the chunk's packet went to that player, so a mod can send its own data; moment, per-player arguments and ordering after `chunk_watch` have no Fabric counterpart. No key. |
| `chunk_unwatch` | **Gap:** none; nearest `ServerChunkEvents.CHUNK_UNLOAD` | `CHUNK_UNLOAD` fires when the server unloads a chunk because its load level rose ([the chunk map mixin][chunk-map-mixin] lines 44-52, declaration at [the server chunk API][server-chunk-api] lines 63-72); it is level state, not one viewer leaving. `native_chunk_unwatch` fires from `run_chunk_watches` for `ChunkWatchKind::Unwatch` after `ChunkMap.dropChunk`, per player and chunk, including a player leaving a level or the server. Different moment and arguments. No key. |
| `client_packet` | **Gap:** none; nearest `ClientReceiveMessageEvents.CHAT` | That event only sees an already-parsed player chat message while `ChatListenerMixin` handles it ([api][receive-api] lines 99-108, [mixin][receive-mixin] lines 57-65). Fabric API 26.3 declares no raw inbound play-packet event. `native_client_packet` runs from `observe_client_packet` for decoded play-phase clientbound packets delivered to the window's game, excluding packets handled by the network thread, passing the packet view; no Fabric event offers that firing point or that argument. No key. |
| `client_packet_send` | **Gap:** none; nearest `ClientSendMessageEvents.CHAT` and `ALLOW_CHAT` | `ALLOW_CHAT`/`CHAT` fire at `ClientPacketListener.sendChat` HEAD with only the chat string, and `ALLOW_CHAT` can veto that one message ([the send API][send-api] lines 29-37 and 100-106, [the send mixin][send-mixin] lines 34-42). `native_client_packet_send` runs from `client_packet_send_canceled` before play-phase serverbound packets sent by the window's game, excluding network-thread and connection-internal sends, with a packet view and a mutable cancel flag; a string-only chat veto cannot stand in for arbitrary packets and their data. No key. |
| `client_pause_change_pre` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no client pause or game-pause event. `native_client_pause_change_pre` fires in `handle_window_event_inner` when `pauses_game()` differs from the current pause, before the new state is stored, with the proposed pause and a mutable cancel; nothing in Fabric sits at that decision. No key. |
| `client_pause_change_post` | **Gap:** none | As above, Fabric API 26.3 declares no pause-change event. `native_client_pause_change_post` fires in the same `handle_window_event_inner` branch right after `self.pause` was updated and before the integrated server is told, with the new pause value; no Fabric event carries that notification. No key. |
## Event counterparts: miscellaneous client, menu and block-query hooks (#1014)

Fabric evidence is at `2065040b236a0d5ac011bd6a45625dc49579b222` (`fabric-26.3/`); Horizon call sites are named by function, never by line. Scope: the eight hooks `client_player_change_game_type`, `client_player_clone`, `client_disconnecting`, `menu_background`, `clone_item_stack`, `compute_fov_modifier`, `connects_to` and `contents_changed`. All eight record a gap and no keys are added.

| Horizon hook | Fabric event considered / status | Evidence and porting boundary |
|---|---|---|
| `client_player_change_game_type` | **Gap:** none; nearest `ClientReceiveMessageEvents.ALLOW_GAME`, `GAME` and `GAME_CANCELED` | Those fire while an arriving server message is displayed or vetoed, invoked from `ChatListenerMixin` in `fabric_allowSystemMessage` and `fabric_allowOverlayMessage` ([the receive API][receive-api] lines 57-71, 114-123 and 138-143; [the receive mixin][receive-mixin] lines 66-89). `native_client_player_change_game_type` instead fires from the player-info update loop in `handle_packet` when one entry's `game_mode` differs from the tracked mode for the local profile, carrying player id, uuid, current and new game type. Fabric 26.3 has no game-mode-change event, and the message events carry no such arguments. No key. |
| `client_player_clone` | **Gap:** none; nearest `ClientPlayConnectionEvents.JOIN` and `DISCONNECT` | `JOIN` and `DISCONNECT` are declared at [the client connection API][client-conn] lines 48-64 and fire when a play listener is ready or its connection is discarded, not when the client replaces its own player. `native_client_player_clone` fires from `handle_packet` after a respawn packet entered the new level, with the old and new entity id, uuid, died and dimension_changed. No Fabric event covers player replacement or carries those arguments. No key. |
| `client_disconnecting` | **Gap:** `ClientPlayConnectionEvents.DISCONNECT` | Fabric invokes `DISCONNECT` with a `ClientPacketListener` and `Minecraft` ([the client connection API][client-conn] lines 59-63 and 79-81), from `invokeDisconnectEvent` ([the play addon][play-addon] lines 96-99); `ConnectionMixin` enters disconnect handling at `channelInactive` HEAD or before `PacketListener.onDisconnect` ([the connection mixin][conn-mixin] lines 89-100). `native_client_disconnecting` instead runs from `leave_server` after remote columns and the client-level unload notice, before the remote session is discarded, and supplies no packet listener. The client host does not replace that data argument, and the firing point is not the same connection callback. No key. |
| `menu_background` | **Gap:** none; nearest `ScreenEvents.afterBackground` | `afterBackground` is declared at [the screen events API][screen-events] lines 129-134 and its listener gets the screen, graphics, mouse position and tick progress after a screen's background is extracted (listener at lines 205-206). `native_menu_background` fires in `menu_blur` with a mutable `Option<f32>` blur radius, before finite/range validation, at the menu blur request. Fabric has no blur request or radius event, and its per-screen background event fires at a different moment with no mutable radius. No key. |
| `clone_item_stack` | **Gap:** `PlayerPickItemEvents.BLOCK` | Fabric calls the event before `BlockState.getCloneItemStack`, passing `ServerPlayer`, position, state and the raw packet include-data request ([the pick item API][pick-api] lines 39-49 and 68-82; [the pick item mixin][pick-mixin] lines 55-67). `lan_guest_packet` calls `native_clone_item_stack` after `clone_stack`, without a player argument and with include-data already restricted to creative players; it then applies block data even to a replacement. Fabric skips the vanilla clone query for a non-null answer and prevents vanilla data inclusion for that replacement, so firing point, arguments and replacement behavior differ. No key. |
| `compute_fov_modifier` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no FOV modifier event. `native_compute_fov_modifier` fires in `tick_fov` once per client tick with a player, after vanilla effects and before zoom and easing, carrying the `FovPlayer`, the base modifier and a mutable modifier. Nothing in Fabric sits at that moment or carries those arguments. No key. |
| `connects_to` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no event for fence, wall, iron-bar or pane connection queries. `native_connects_to` fires from `adjust_connects_to` inside the level's shape update, carrying dimension, pos, the block state, its neighbour, direction, face_solid, the connection kind and a mutable connected flag. No Fabric event exists at that call site. No key. |
| `contents_changed` | **Gap:** none; nearest `ServerLifecycleEvents.SYNC_DATA_PACK_CONTENTS` | That event fires when tag and recipe data is sent to a joining player or after a resource reload ([the server lifecycle API][sl-api] lines 80-84; [the player list mixin][sl-sync-mixin] lines 32-49), which is unrelated to a container write. `native_contents_changed` fires from `container_changed` at `Container.setChanged` / `BlockEntity.setChanged`, after the write and before dirty and comparator updates, with the level, pos and block state. Fabric 26.3 declares no container-contents-changed event. No key. |

[client-conn]: https://github.com/FabricMC/fabric/blob/2065040b236a0d5ac011bd6a45625dc49579b222/fabric-networking-api-v1/src/client/java/net/fabricmc/fabric/api/client/networking/v1/ClientPlayConnectionEvents.java
[pick-api]: https://github.com/FabricMC/fabric/blob/2065040b236a0d5ac011bd6a45625dc49579b222/fabric-events-interaction-v0/src/main/java/net/fabricmc/fabric/api/event/player/PlayerPickItemEvents.java
[pick-mixin]: https://github.com/FabricMC/fabric/blob/2065040b236a0d5ac011bd6a45625dc49579b222/fabric-events-interaction-v0/src/main/java/net/fabricmc/fabric/mixin/event/interaction/ServerGamePacketListenerImplMixin.java

## Event counterparts: misc create, crop-growth, custom-spawner and boss-overlay hooks (#1014)

Fabric evidence is at `2065040b236a0d5ac011bd6a45625dc49579b222` (`fabric-26.3/`); Horizon call sites are named by function, never by line. Scope: the eight hooks `create_fluid_source`, `create_spawn_position`, `crop_grow_pre`, `crop_grow_post`, `custom_click_action`, `modify_custom_spawners`, `custom_spawner_tick` and `customize_boss_event_progress`. All eight record a gap and no keys are added.

| Horizon hook | Fabric event considered / status | Evidence and porting boundary |
|---|---|---|
| `create_fluid_source` | **Gap:** none; nearest `FluidFlowEvents.ALLOW` | `ALLOW` is declared in [the fluid flow API][fluid-flow-api] lines 26-30 with `allowFlow(FluidState, LevelAccessor, BlockPos)` (lines 62-73) and is invoked at `spreadTo` HEAD to veto a flow into a position ([FlowingFluidMixin][fluid-flow-mixin] lines 40-48; [LavaFluidMixin][lava-fluid-mixin] lines 36-44). `native_create_fluid_source` instead fires from `create_fluid_source` during the fluid's new-liquid query for each horizontal neighbour source that might count towards conversion, seeded with `can_convert` from the `water_source_conversion`/`lava_source_conversion` rule, carrying dimension, pos and block state, and its mutable `can_convert` can also force conversion where vanilla declined. The moment, arguments and veto-or-force behaviour differ. No key. |
| `create_spawn_position` | **Gap:** none; nearest `EntitySleepEvents.ALLOW_SETTING_SPAWN` and `MODIFY_WAKE_UP_POSITION` | Both are declared in [the sleep API][sleep-api] (lines 170-175 and 201-208) and fire while a living entity sleeps: `ALLOW_SETTING_SPAWN` gates `setRespawnPosition` in [ServerPlayerMixin][base-block-server-player-mixin] lines 102-110, and `MODIFY_WAKE_UP_POSITION` redirects the wake-up search in [LivingEntityMixin][bc-living-mixin] lines 166-174. `native_create_spawn_position` fires from `find_world_spawn` once before a new level's initial world-spawn search, carrying mutable position and rotation plus a cancel that substitutes the level spawn. Neither sleep event sits at that moment or carries rotation and cancel. No key. |
| `crop_grow_pre` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no crop-growth event; the event index has no crop, grow or random-tick block event. `native_crop_grow_pre` fires before a crop-like block grows on a random tick and offers a tri-state result that can leave vanilla's roll, force growth or stop it, with dimension, pos and the growing state. Nothing in Fabric sits before growth or carries that answer. No key. |
| `crop_grow_post` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no post-growth event either. `native_crop_grow_post` fires after a crop-like block grew, carrying the dimension, pos, the state before growth and the state now, with sugar cane and vine heads reported at the new block. Both arguments and the moment are Horizon-only. No key. |
| `custom_click_action` | **Gap:** none; nearest `ItemClickBehaviorCallback.EVENT` | That event fires at `AbstractContainerMenu.tryItemClickBehaviourOverride` HEAD for a slot click, with both item stacks, the slots, the click action and the player, on client and server ([the item click API][item-click-api] lines 29-80; [AbstractContainerMenuMixin][item-click-mixin] lines 33-41). `native_custom_click_action` fires when a server player's `custom` click action arrives from the client, carrying player id, uuid, name, the action id and an optional payload, and cancels only vanilla's log line. `EntityElytraEvents.CUSTOM` and `ScreenMouseEvents.afterMouseClick` are elytra flight and client mouse input, unrelated. No key. |
| `modify_custom_spawners` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no event for a level's custom spawner list; the index has no spawner event. `native_modify_custom_spawners` builds a dimension's `CustomSpawners` once, before vanilla spawners are consulted, and passes the dimension id with the mutable list. No Fabric event exposes that list or fires at that point. No key. |
| `custom_spawner_tick` | **Gap:** none | This hook is already beyond NeoForge: a `SpawnerKey` stands in for a `CustomSpawner` object, and `native_custom_spawner_tick` is fired once per tick per mod key with the dimension and `spawn_enemies`. Fabric API 26.3 exposes no event for ticking entries in a level's custom-spawner list. No key. |
| `customize_boss_event_progress` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no boss-bar layout event (the index has no boss-bar entry); its `HudElement.extractRenderState` callback receives only graphics and timing context ([the HUD element API][hud-element] lines 28-36), with no per-bar position or increment. `native_customize_boss_event_progress` is asked once per frame per boss bar before drawing, with mutable x, y and increment plus a cancel that suppresses just that bar. The moment and the mutable layout arguments have no Fabric counterpart. No key. |

## Event counterparts: misc customize hooks (#1014)

Fabric evidence is at `2065040b236a0d5ac011bd6a45625dc49579b222` (`fabric-26.3/`); Horizon call sites are named by function, never by line. Scope: the eight hooks `customize_chat_overlay`, `debug_entry_lines`, `debug_renderer_draw`, `effect_particle_modification`, `enchanted_block_loot`, `enchanted_entity_loot`, `enchantment_level_set` and `ender_entity_teleport`. All eight record a gap and no keys are added.

| Horizon hook | Fabric event considered / status | Evidence and porting boundary |
|---|---|---|
| `customize_chat_overlay` | **Gap:** none; nearest `ClientReceiveMessageEvents.ALLOW_CHAT` and `CHAT` | `ALLOW_CHAT` is declared in [the receive-message API][receive-api] lines 39-47 as `allowReceiveChatMessage(Component, PlayerChatMessage, GameProfile, ChatType.Bound, Instant)` and `CHAT` in the same file lines 99-108; both are invoked at the chat listener's HEAD while a received message is decorated, before it reaches the chat ([ChatListenerMixin][receive-mixin] lines 56-64), and `ALLOW_CHAT` only vetoes display. `native_customize_chat_overlay` is instead asked from `draw_chat` once a frame the chat layer draws, with the GUI width and height, the focused flag, and mutable `x`/`y` for the chat's bottom-left corner. The HUD callback `HudElement.extractRenderState` receives only graphics and timing context ([the HUD element API][hud-element] lines 28-35), not mutable chat coordinates. The moment and mutable position have no matching Fabric contract. No key. |
| `debug_entry_lines` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no debug-overlay entry event; the event index has no debug entry, debug renderer or F3 event. `native_debug_entry_lines` fires from `debug_entry_lines` while the overlay draws a mod-registered entry whose toggle is enabled, carrying the entry id and mutable lines. Nothing in Fabric reaches a mod-registered debug entry. No key. |
| `debug_renderer_draw` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no per-debug-renderer draw event, and the event index has no debug renderer entry. `native_debug_renderer_draw` fires from `fill_world_geometry` for each active renderer id after custom geometry is submitted, with the id, the world render view and a mutable `WorldGeometry`. The nearest `LevelRenderEvents.BEFORE_GIZMOS` receives only `LevelRenderContext` ([the level-render API][level-render-events] lines 125-135 and 214-215) and fires before `finalizeGizmoCollection` ([the level-render mixin][level-renderer-mixin] lines 117-120), not once per active renderer id with this mutable geometry buffer. No key. |
| `effect_particle_modification` | **Gap:** none; nearest `ServerMobEffectEvents.AFTER_ADD` and `AFTER_REMOVE` | `AFTER_ADD` and `AFTER_REMOVE` are declared in [the mob effect API][effect-api] lines 79-86 and 136-143 and fire after an effect was added to or removed from a living entity, with the `MobEffectInstance` and entity ([LivingEntityMixin][effect-mixin] lines 98-106 and 213-221); `ALLOW_EARLY_REMOVE` (lines 92-111) only vetoes a removal. `native_effect_particle_modification` instead fires while a level's visible effect particles are collected for an entity, carrying the active effect, the original particle and mutable particle and visibility. The add/remove moment, the veto-only answer and the particle arguments do not match. No key. |
| `enchanted_block_loot` | **Gap:** none; nearest `LootTableEvents.MODIFY_DROPS` | `MODIFY_DROPS` is declared in [the loot API][lt-api] lines 105-110 and is invoked after `getRandomItemsRaw` has produced the drops, with the table holder, the loot context and the resulting stack list ([LootTableMixin][lt-table-mixin] lines 49-57); `ALL_LOADED` and `MODIFY` run at table load. `native_enchanted_block_loot` fires from `roll_block_loot` in the middle of the roll, each time an enchantment's level is read for the block's table, carrying dimension, pos, block state, tool, optional entity, the enchantment and a mutable level. The moment and the mutable level argument differ. No key. |
| `enchanted_entity_loot` | **Gap:** none; nearest `LootTableEvents.MODIFY_DROPS` | The same event (declaration in [the loot API][lt-api] lines 105-110, invocation in [LootTableMixin][lt-table-mixin] lines 49-57) only sees the finished drop list after the roll. `native_enchanted_entity_loot` fires from `enchanted_level` in the middle of the roll, each time an enchanted-count function or enchanted-bonus condition reads a level, carrying the dropping entity, the hurt source, the attacker, the enchantment and a mutable level. Neither the moment nor the entity/attacker/enchantment arguments are covered. No key. |
| `enchantment_level_set` | **Gap:** none; nearest `EnchantmentEvents.ALLOW_ENCHANTING` and `MODIFY` | `ALLOW_ENCHANTING` is a tri-state `allowEnchanting(Holder<Enchantment>, ItemStack, EnchantingContext)` in [the enchantment API][enchant-events-api] lines 35-54 invoked by `FabricItemStack.canBeEnchantedWith` to decide applicability ([the item-stack API][bc-item-stack-api] lines 59-66); `MODIFY` (lines 69-84) is invoked while an enchantment definition is loaded ([EnchantmentUtil][enchant-util] lines 61-69). `native_enchantment_level_set` instead fires from `lan_enchant_offers` after `getEnchantmentCost` for each enchanting-table row, carrying the table pos, the player, row, power, item and a mutable level. No event sits at the table's offer draw or carries the level. No key. |
| `ender_entity_teleport` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 has no event for an enderman's or shulker's own random teleport; `ServerEntityLevelChangeEvents.AFTER_ENTITY_CHANGE_LEVEL` is a notification with original/new entities and origin/destination levels ([the level-change API][level-change-api] lines 32-46), invoked only after a successful `teleportCrossDimension` ([the entity mixin][level-change-mixin] lines 37-44). `native_ender_entity_teleport` fires from `teleport_to` (enderman) and `teleport` (shulker) after the passenger guard and before ground/collision checks for the enderman, and after random candidate selection, collision and attachment checks for the shulker, with the entity, kind, from, a mutable target and a cancel. The moment, the mutable target and the cancel have no Fabric counterpart. No key. |

[enchant-util]: https://github.com/FabricMC/fabric/blob/2065040b236a0d5ac011bd6a45625dc49579b222/fabric-item-api-v1/src/main/java/net/fabricmc/fabric/impl/item/EnchantmentUtil.java

## Event counterparts: misc enderman hooks (#1014)

Fabric evidence is at `2065040b236a0d5ac011bd6a45625dc49579b222` (`fabric-26.3/`); Horizon call sites are named by function, never by line. Scope: the eight hooks `enderman_anger`, `entity_change_block_pre`, `entity_constructing`, `entity_inside`, `entity_interact_specific`, `entity_mob_griefing`, `entity_outline` and `entity_place`. All eight record a gap and no keys are added.

| Horizon hook | Fabric event considered / status | Evidence and porting boundary |
|---|---|---|
| `enderman_anger` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no enderman-stare or anger event, and the event index has no enderman, stare or anger entry. `enderman_anger_suppressed` asks Horizon's own hook from the enderman goals, with the enderman, the player and a mutable cancel, so there is no Fabric firing point to attach it to. No key. |
| `entity_change_block_pre` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 has no event for an entity changing a block, and the event index has no `EntityChangeBlock` entry. `landing_change_cancelled` fires while Horizon handles a landing with the player, the position, the from/to block names and a mutable cancel; the block-write moment and the name arguments have no Fabric counterpart. No key. |
| `entity_constructing` | **Gap:** none; nearest `ServerEntityEvents.ENTITY_LOAD` | Fabric API 26.3 declares no entity-construction event. `ENTITY_LOAD` receives the entity and level after the entity is already in the level ([the server entity API][sentity-api] lines 35-46), firing at `onTrackingStart` TAIL ([the entity callbacks mixin][sentity-callbacks-mixin] lines 38-40). `initialize_entity` fires Horizon's hook on both sides before attachments are read, carrying the holder and type name; the loading moment, server-only side and entity/level arguments do not match. No key. |
| `entity_inside` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 has no event for `Block.entityInside`, and the event index has no entity-inside entry. `drain_entity_inside` fires Horizon's hook after its contact batch with the block state, position, entity and `precise` flag; no Fabric moment carries those. No key. |
| `entity_interact_specific` | **Gap:** none; nearest `UseEntityCallback.EVENT` | The event is declared in [the use-entity API][usecb-api] lines 47-62 as `interact(Player, Level, InteractionHand, Entity, EntityHitResult)`; the same file documents that it is hooked in before the spectator check (line 31) and gives return meanings for both logical sides (lines 33-45). Its server firing point is `ServerGamePacketListenerImpl.handleInteract` before the held-stack read, where it builds the hit result from the packet's relative location and cancels on any non-`PASS` answer ([the server interaction mixin][usecb-mixin] lines 83-93). `native_entity_interact_specific` is server-side and carries the held `ItemStack` itself and the clicked point relative to the entity, so the event's hand instead of the stack, entity object instead of an id and absolute hit location leave the arguments different; Horizon's claimed answer also swings the hand, whereas the Fabric server callback only stops further processing on non-`PASS`. No key. |
| `entity_mob_griefing` | **Gap:** none; nearest `ServerLivingEntityEvents.MOB_CONVERSION` and `ServerMobEffectEvents.BEFORE_ADD` | `MOB_CONVERSION` is declared in [the living-entity API][bc-living-api] lines 94-103 with the listener at line 164, and `BEFORE_ADD` in [the mob-effect API][effect-api] lines 64-73 with the listener at line 178, fired before an effect is added ([the effect mixin][effect-mixin] lines 70-78). Both are different moments (a mob's conversion, an effect addition) and neither carries the boolean `can_grief` that `entity_grief` asks from the `mob_griefing` rule and lets the mod answer. No key. |
| `entity_outline` | **Gap:** none; nearest `LevelExtractionEvents.AFTER_BLOCK_OUTLINE_EXTRACTION` and `LevelRenderEvents.BEFORE_BLOCK_OUTLINE` | `AFTER_BLOCK_OUTLINE_EXTRACTION` is declared in [the level-extraction API][level-extraction-events] lines 31-57 and fires at the return of `extractBlockOutline` ([the extractor mixin][level-extractor-mixin] lines 60-68); `BEFORE_BLOCK_OUTLINE` (declared lines 97-114, listener at line 209) only vetoes the block outline render from `submitBlockOutline` ([the level-render mixin][level-renderer-mixin] lines 106-114). Both concern the block the crosshair hits; `entity_outline` is asked per drawn entity and replaces its glowing colour with `Some`/`None`. Neither the moment nor the mutable entity outline and colour match. No key. |
| `entity_place` | **Gap:** none; nearest `UseBlockCallback.EVENT` | `UseBlockCallback.EVENT` receives a player, level, hand and block hit result ([the block-use API][use-block-api] lines 38-52), firing at `useItemOn` HEAD and stopping further processing for non-`PASS` ([the server game-mode mixin][spgm-mixin] lines 85-93), before a placement is determined. `place_canceled` and `player_place_canceled` fire Horizon's hook with the placer, the primary position and state, the state placed against and the whole `Placement` (`Single`, or every block of a `Multi`), cancelling the write. The block-use moment also includes non-placement interactions and excludes mob placements; it carries neither the placement nor its multi-block form. No key. |

## Event counterparts: misc entity hooks (#1014)

Fabric evidence is at `2065040b236a0d5ac011bd6a45625dc49579b222` (`fabric-26.3/`); Horizon call sites are named by function, never by line. Scope: the nine hooks `entity_struck_by_lightning`, `entity_travel_to_dimension`, `explosion_knockback`, `explosion_start`, `extract_block_screen_effect`, `farmland_trample`, `finalize_spawn`, `fluid_flow` and `fluid_place_block`. All nine record a gap and no keys are added.

| Horizon hook | Fabric event considered / status | Evidence and porting boundary |
|---|---|---|
| `entity_struck_by_lightning` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no lightning-strike event, and the event index has no lightning, struck or thunder entry. `lightning_may_strike` asks Horizon's hook once per bolt for each entity whose box meets the strike box, before `Entity.thunderHit`, carrying the entity and the bolt with a mutable cancel that also suppresses fire and mob conversion. No Fabric event sits at that moment or carries the bolt. No key. |
| `entity_travel_to_dimension` | **Gap:** nearest `ServerEntityLevelChangeEvents.AFTER_ENTITY_CHANGE_LEVEL` and `AFTER_PLAYER_CHANGE_LEVEL` | [The level-change API][level-change-api] lines 32-63 declares only after-the-fact notifications for entities and players, carrying the original and new entity and both levels, with no cause and no veto; they fire after the move completes. `entity_may_travel` asks Horizon's hook before anything moves, for a server player or another entity about to take a Nether portal, End portal, End gateway or a level-changing `/tp` or spectator teleport, with the entity handle, the target dimension id, a cause (`nether_portal`, `end_portal`, `end_gateway` or `teleport`) and a mutable cancel. Moment, cause argument and control differ. No key. |
| `explosion_knockback` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no explosion event, and the event index has no explosion or knockback entry. `explosion_hurt_entities` and `explosion_hurt_player` call Horizon's hook inside the hurt pass after the knockback vector is computed, with the dimension, centre, broken blocks, an optional entity id and the mutable velocity. No Fabric event sits inside the blast or exposes the velocity. No key. |
| `explosion_start` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no explosion-start event, and the event index has no explosion entry. `explosion_start_cancelled` asks Horizon's hook before a server explosion runs, with the native explosion-start data and a cancel that skips blocks, damage, sound and particles and the later `explosion_prime`. No Fabric event precedes `ServerExplosion.explode` with that data or a cancel. No key. |
| `extract_block_screen_effect` | **Gap:** none; nearest `LevelExtractionEvents.END_EXTRACTION` | `END_EXTRACTION` is declared in [level-extraction-events] line 70 and fired at the return of `extract` in [level-extractor-mixin] line 71, once for the whole extraction pass. `screen_effect_kept` asks Horizon's hook per block screen effect with the effect kind, block state, position and partial tick, and receives both a cancel and a custom-draw answer. No Fabric event runs per screen effect or carries those values. No key. |
| `farmland_trample` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no farmland-trample or landing event, and the event index has no farmland, trample or landing entry. `cancel_farmland_trample` fires Horizon's hook from the player and mob landing paths before the block is written, with the entity id, position, state and fall distance and a mutable cancel. No Fabric event sits in the landing. No key. |
| `finalize_spawn` | **Gap:** none; nearest `ServerEntityEvents.ENTITY_LOAD` | `ENTITY_LOAD` receives the entity and level after the entity is already in the level ([sentity-api] lines 35-46), firing at `onTrackingStart` TAIL ([sentity-callbacks-mixin] lines 38-40). `finalize_spawn_lent` asks Horizon's hook before a mob is finalized, with the dimension, kind, reason, position, spawner, mutable difficulty, the mob, group, and cancel/skip answers. The moment and the mutable spawn data do not match. No key. |
| `fluid_flow` | **Gap:** none; nearest `FluidFlowEvents.ALLOW` | `ALLOW` is declared in [fluid-flow-api] lines 26-30 (listener line 62) and fired at `spreadTo` HEAD in [fluid-flow-mixin] lines 40-48 and [lava-fluid-mixin] lines 36-44, with another veto at `shouldSpreadLiquid` HEAD ([liquid-block-mixin] lines 39-43), before the liquid block checks its neighbours. The event receives a fluid state, level and one position and can only veto further handling. `native_fluid_flow` instead fires from `pushing_flow` after `flow_at` computes `FlowingFluid.getFlow`, with the fluid name, position and a mutable flow vector, so mods edit the push applied to an entity. Moment, arguments and the veto-versus-listen contract differ. No key. |
| `fluid_place_block` | **Gap:** none; nearest `FluidFlowEvents.ALLOW` | The same `ALLOW` event ([fluid-flow-api] lines 26-30, listener line 62; [fluid-flow-mixin] lines 40-48 and [lava-fluid-mixin] lines 36-44) vetoes `spreadTo` before a fluid flows into a position, and also fires at `shouldSpreadLiquid` HEAD ([liquid-block-mixin] lines 39-43), before neighbour-driven liquid block formation is determined. `fluid_place_block` fires Horizon's hook when liquid at `liquid_pos` is about to place `state` at `pos`, with both positions, the state and a mutable replacement state plus a cancel. `ALLOW` carries no target block state and cannot replace it. No key. |
## Event counterparts: misc gather hooks (#1014)

Fabric evidence is at `2065040b236a0d5ac011bd6a45625dc49579b222` (`fabric-26.3/`); Horizon call sites are named by function, never by line. Scope: the eight hooks `gather_skipped_attribute_tooltips`, `gather_effect_screen_tooltips`, `game_rule_changed`, `game_shutting_down`, `get_arm_pose`, `get_enchantment_level`, `grindstone_place` and `grindstone_take`. All eight record a gap and no keys are added.

| Horizon hook | Fabric event considered / status | Evidence and porting boundary |
|---|---|---|
| `gather_skipped_attribute_tooltips` | **Gap:** none; nearest `ItemTooltipCallback.EVENT` | `EVENT` is declared in [item-tooltip-api] lines 30-33 and fired at the second `getTooltipLines` RETURN in [item-tooltip-mixin] lines 34-42, after every base line has been appended, with the stack, tooltip context, flag and the finished line list. Horizon asks its hook from `skipped` before `addAttributeTooltips` builds the attribute lines, per stack, with the player entity id, the advanced/creative flags and mutable `skip_all`, skipped modifier ids and skipped slot groups. No Fabric event runs at that moment or carries that filter. No key. |
| `gather_effect_screen_tooltips` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no event for the inventory effect panel's tooltip, and the event index has no effect-tooltip entry. `effect_tooltip` asks Horizon's hook per hovered, rendered panel with the screen view, the active effect and the mutable coloured line list. The nearest candidates are `ClientTooltipComponentCallback.EVENT` ([client-tooltip-component-api] lines 28-37, fired at `ClientTooltipComponent.create` HEAD in [client-tooltip-component-mixin] lines 31-39) for item tooltip components and the server-side `ServerMobEffectEvents.AFTER_ADD`/`AFTER_REMOVE` ([effect-api] lines 79-86 and 136-143, fired at `onEffectAdded`/`onEffectsRemoved` RETURN in [effect-mixin] lines 98-106 and 213-221); none sits at the panel tooltip or carries the effect with the line list. No key. |
| `game_rule_changed` | **Gap:** none; nearest `GameRuleEvents.changeCallback` | Fabric's per-rule `changeCallback` returns a `ValueUpdate` event ([gamerule-events-api] lines 32-52) fired at `MinecraftServer.onGameRuleChanged` RETURN ([gamerule-mixin] lines 33-39) with the typed value and the server only; the rule is implicit in the registration and no actor is passed. `game_rule_changed` reports the rule name, the value and the optional `GameRuleActor` that caused the change, so its actor argument has no Fabric counterpart. No key. |
| `game_shutting_down` | **Gap:** none; nearest `ServerLifecycleEvents.SERVER_STOPPED` | `SERVER_STOPPED` is declared in [server-lifecycle-api] line 74 and fired at `stopServer` TAIL in [server-lifecycle-mixin] lines 72-74, after the server has stopped and on the server side only. Horizon's hook is `side = "both"`: `start_game` and `run_event_loop` call it once for the dedicated server and once for the client after the game loop returns, and Fabric 26.3 offers only the pre-exit `ClientLifecycleEvents.CLIENT_STOPPING` ([cl-api] line 45, fired inside `exitWorldAndClose` after its log call in [cl-mixin] lines 44-46), no client counterpart at that moment. No key. |
| `get_arm_pose` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no arm-pose event and the event index has no arm or pose entry. `held_arm_poses` asks Horizon's hook per player model and non-empty hand, with the entity id, hand, stack and a mutable pose that replaces what the use animation, a loaded crossbow or the item's own declaration give. No Fabric event runs at `AvatarRenderer.getArmPose` or offers a replaceable pose. No key. |
| `get_enchantment_level` | **Gap:** none; nearest `EnchantmentEvents.ALLOW_ENCHANTING` | `ALLOW_ENCHANTING` is declared in [enchant-events-api] lines 35-54 and returns a tri-state allow, deny or default answer about whether an enchantment may be applied to a stack ([enchant-events-api] lines 54-67), while `MODIFY`/`MODIFY_WITH_LOOKUP` edit a registered enchantment definition ([enchant-events-api] lines 69-107, [enchant-util] lines 61-70). `get_enchantment_level` fires from `install_stack_hooks` on every `ItemStack` enchantment-level read, with the stack, an optional target enchantment and the mutable level map. No Fabric event intercepts that read or exposes the levels. No key. |
| `grindstone_place` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no grindstone event, and the event index has no grindstone entry; the only grindstone references are item tag datagen. `api_grindstone_place` asks Horizon's hook from `GrindstoneMenu.createResult` when the inputs change, with the player UUID and entity id, the position, the mutable inputs/result/xp and a cancel. No Fabric event sits in that menu's result creation. No key. |
| `grindstone_take` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no grindstone event, and the event index has no grindstone entry. `api_grindstone_take` asks Horizon's hook from the result slot's `onTake`, with the player UUID and entity id, the position, the mutable xp and remaining inputs, and a cancel that skips xp, sound and input consumption. No Fabric event covers that take. No key. |

## Event counterparts: misc hand hooks (#1014)

Fabric evidence is at `2065040b236a0d5ac011bd6a45625dc49579b222` (`fabric-26.3/`); Horizon call sites are named by function, never by line. Scope: the eight hooks `damage_item`, `hand_drawn`, `ingredient_test`, `is_pathfindable`, `item_after_vertical_collision`, `item_attribute_modifier`, `item_fished` and `item_interact_mob`, plus the registrar's item-container declaration. All record a gap and no keys are added.

| Horizon hook | Fabric event considered / status | Evidence and porting boundary |
|---|---|---|
| `damage_item` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no durability or `ItemStack.hurtAndBreak` event and the pinned index lists nothing for it; `PlayerBlockBreakEvents` covers block breaking only. Horizon's `durability_loss` asks the `ItemPart`s of the worn item (`IItemExtension.damageItem`) after Unbreaking, with the stack, a mutable loss and a cancel that leaves the item whole, on every wear path (a player's tools, armour and shield, a dispenser's tool, a mob's armour) and skips creative tools and saved items. No Fabric event fires at that moment or carries the mutable amount. No key. |
| `hand_drawn` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no first-person hand-render event and the event index has no hand or `RenderHandEvent` entry. `hand_drawn` is asked on every client frame from the first-person hand draw, with no hand too, and no Fabric event runs at that moment or exposes the draw. No key. |
| `ingredient_test` | **Gap:** none; nearest `CustomIngredient` (not an event) | Fabric's recipe API lets a mod supply a `CustomIngredient` whose own `test` decides a match ([hand-custom-ingredient-api] lines 54-62), but that is an interface, not an event, and no Fabric callback runs at Horizon's moment. `test_in` calls `native_ingredient_test` from the ingredient's own `test` after the load-time item list has matched and `accept` is already true, passing the loop context, kind, JSON and stack and taking back a mutable `accept`. No key. |
| `is_pathfindable` | **Gap:** none; nearest `LandPathTypeRegistry` (not an event) | Fabric 26.3 declares no pathfindable event; `LandPathTypeRegistry` registers static or dynamic providers of a `PathType`, including providers that depend on level and position ([hand-path-type-api] lines 73-107), a different question from Horizon's boolean per block state and `PathComputationType`. `resolve_pathfindable` asks `native_is_pathfindable` when parts resolve, and the stored answer may not depend on position, level or time. No key. |
| `item_after_vertical_collision` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no dropped-item vertical-contact event and the event index has no item-entity collision entry. `tick_item_with_block_contacts` calls `native_item_after_vertical_collision` after vertical collision response and before block effects, merging and pickup, with the selected block state, item position, stack and a mutable discard. No Fabric event sits at that point. No key. |
| `item_attribute_modifier` | **Gap:** none; nearest `FabricDefaultAttributeRegistry.MODIFY` and `DimensionEvents.MODIFY_ATTRIBUTES` | `MODIFY` bulk-edits default attribute suppliers per entity type when registries are frozen ([default-attribute-api] lines 48-62), and `MODIFY_ATTRIBUTES` edits a dimension's environment attributes ([dimension-events-api] lines 31-35); neither sees a stack or a modifier list. `install_stack_hooks` calls `native_item_attribute_modifier` on every stack attribute-modifier read, with the stack and mutable entries. No key. |
| `Registrar::item_container` | **Gap:** none; nearest `DefaultItemComponentEvents.MODIFY` | `MODIFY` edits an item type's default component map during registration ([default-item-component-events] line 38), not its live container capability. Horizon registers an `ItemContainerProvider` for an `ItemKey`; its `slots(&dyn Host)` callback remains dynamic and contents live in `minecraft:container`. Registration-time type coverage reads the declaration without invoking the callback, even when it would currently return `None`. No event key. |
| `item_fished` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no fishing event and the event index has no fishing entry. `retrieve_hook` calls `native_item_fished` after the fishing loot has rolled, with the bobber, the caught items, the mutable rod damage and a cancel; no Fabric event runs at that moment or carries those arguments. No key. Diverges: Horizon lets a listener change the drop list itself; the reference lets a listener change the stacks but not the list. |
| `item_interact_mob` | **Gap:** none; nearest `UseEntityCallback.EVENT` | `EVENT` fires before the spectator check and before the interaction is routed, on client `startUseItem` and server `handleInteract` ([use-entity-api] lines 40-63, [use-entity-server-mixin] line 88), with the player, level, hand, entity and hit result and returning an `InteractionResult`. `item_interact_mob` runs server-side after ordinary interactions pass, only for an alive native mob selected by packet id or crosshair, with the acting stack and a mutable `Option<bool>` swing. The firing point, side, arguments and cancel shape all differ. No key. |
