## Event counterparts: misc item hooks (#1014)

Fabric evidence is at `2065040b236a0d5ac011bd6a45625dc49579b222` (`fabric-26.3/`); Horizon call sites are named by function, never by line. Scope: the eight hooks `item_smelted`, `item_stacked_on_other`, `item_toss`, `key_mapping_pre`, `left_click_empty`, `level_save`, `play_level_sound_at_position` and `play_level_sound_at_entity`. All eight record a gap and no keys are added.

| Horizon hook | Fabric event considered / status | Evidence and porting boundary |
|---|---|---|
| `item_smelted` | **Gap:** none | The event index has no smelting, furnace-result or `checkTakeAchievements` entry; none covers taking a furnace result. `native_item_smelted` is called from `lan_machine_click` with the guest's UUID and entity id, the taken stack and the count vanilla removed, at the moment `FurnaceResultSlot.checkTakeAchievements` runs; no Fabric event fires there or carries those arguments. No key. |
| `item_stacked_on_other` | **Gap:** nearest `ItemClickBehaviorCallback.EVENT` | `EVENT` is injected at HEAD of `AbstractContainerMenu.tryItemClickBehaviourOverride` ([item-click-mixin] line 37) and gets the hovered stack and slot, the carried stack, a `SlotAccess` for it, the `ClickAction` and the `Player` as objects ([item-click-api] lines 45-54). Horizon's `item_stacked_on_other` fires at the top of its own menu `pickup` path, before `bundle_click`, with a player UUID and entity id, a container and slot index, a boolean primary flag and mutable carried/clicked views; the data is IDs and views, not the objects, and the moment is Horizon's pickup entry rather than the vanilla override call. Fabric uses `PASS` to continue item overrides and non-`PASS` to return a handled boolean ([item-click-mixin] lines 46-48); Horizon likewise skips `bundle_click` when canceled and uses `cancellation_result` as its handled answer. The argument mismatch alone prevents a key. No key. |
| `item_toss` | **Gap:** none | The event index has no event for a player tossing an item; loot-drop modification is a different moment. `toss_item` calls `native_item_toss` between making the item entity and adding it to the level, only on the non-random `Player.drop` paths, passing the player id and the item entity's handle and taking back a cancel; Fabric offers no event at that moment. No key. |
| `key_mapping_pre` | **Gap:** none; nearest `ScreenKeyboardEvents` | `allowKeyPress`, `beforeKeyPress` and `afterKeyPress` are per-screen events, asked only while a screen handles the key, with the raw `KeyEvent` ([screen-keyboard-events] lines 44-71). Horizon's `key_mapping_canceled` answers only when `gameplay_has_keyboard` holds, that is with no screen open, and asks once per gameplay mapping bound to the input with the mapping's translation key and a pressed flag, dropping the press when canceled. Different moment, different arguments. No key. |
| `left_click_empty` | **Gap:** nearest `ClientPreAttackCallback.EVENT`; also `AttackBlockCallback.EVENT` / `AttackEntityCallback.EVENT` | `ClientPreAttackCallback` runs in client `handleKeybinds` before attack handling whenever the attack key is held or clicked, without requiring an empty target; it receives the client, player and click count and can cancel attacks ([base-on-pre-attack-api] lines 24-61, [base-on-pre-attack-mixin] lines 97-111). `remote_start_attack` calls `native_left_click_empty` only when no entity is targeted and the picked block is air, with no arguments and no cancel. The block/entity callbacks target blocks/entities rather than empty space ([attackblock-api] lines 29-63, [attackentity-api] lines 31-56); their signatures carry player, level, hand and target data, not a stack. No key. |
| `level_save` | **Gap:** nearest `ServerLifecycleEvents.AFTER_SAVE` | `AFTER_SAVE` is injected at TAIL of `MinecraftServer.saveAllChunks` ([server-mixin] line 121) and runs once per server save with the server, `flush` and `force` ([sl-api] lines 119-122, 195). Horizon's `save_level` calls `native_level_save` once for each dimension after scheduling that level's chunk save and saving entities and data, flushing only when `wait` is true, passing the dimension id, on the autosave, `/save-all`, pause, close and stop paths alike. The moment is per level rather than per server save and the arguments differ. No key. |
| `play_level_sound_at_position` | **Gap:** none | The event index has no level-sound or sound-play entry. `ask_level_sound` calls `native_play_level_sound_at_position` for both server and client requests, passing the level and position with the sound, source, volume and pitch all mutable, the original volume and pitch, and a cancel; no Fabric callback runs at that moment or carries those arguments. No key. |
| `play_level_sound_at_entity` | **Gap:** none | The same absence applies to the entity overload: the index lists no sound event, and the `ClientPlayConnectionEvents` entries the name search surfaced are connection lifecycle events. `ask_level_sound` calls `native_play_level_sound_at_entity` when an emitter resolves, with the entity, mutable sound, source, volume and pitch, the originals and a cancel; Fabric has no counterpart at that moment. No key. |
| `viewed_level_sound` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 has no other level on a client and no level-sound event; Immersive Portals' own replay (`CrossPortalSound`) is a mod's. Horizon's client asks it for each sound of a level the server opened for viewing (`horizon:levels` revision 3), with the viewed level, the sound, its source, position, volume, pitch and seed, and an out `play_at` that places it in the player's level. No key. |
## Event counterparts: living entity hooks (#1014)

Fabric evidence is at `2065040b236a0d5ac011bd6a45625dc49579b222` (`fabric-26.3/`); Horizon call sites are named by function, never by line. Scope: the eleven hooks `living_conversion_post`, `living_conversion_pre`, `living_damage_pre`, `living_destroy_block`, `living_get_projectile`, `living_jump_power`, `living_speed`, `living_flying_speed`, `living_shield_block`, `living_use_totem` and `living_visibility`. All eleven record a gap and no keys are added.

| Horizon hook | Fabric event considered / status | Evidence and porting boundary |
|---|---|---|
| `living_conversion_post` | **Gap:** `ServerLivingEntityEvents.MOB_CONVERSION` | Fired from a `@ModifyArg` on `ServerLevel.addFreshEntity` inside `Mob.convertTo`, before the converted mob joins the level, with the previous and converted `Mob` instances and `ConversionParams` ([mob-conversion-mixin] lines 28-36; [living-api] lines 94-103, 164-172). `native_living_conversion_post` runs only after `native_mobs_joined`, that is after the join, and passes entity ids and kind strings rather than the mob objects. Different firing point and arguments. No key. |
| `living_conversion_pre` | **Gap:** `ServerLivingEntityEvents.MOB_CONVERSION` | The event is invoked after the converted `Mob` has been created, immediately before it is added, and its listener returns nothing so it cannot refuse the conversion ([mob-conversion-mixin] lines 28-36; [living-api] lines 94-103, 164-172). `native_living_conversion_pre` runs before the new mob exists and returns a cancel that keeps the old mob, and it passes ids and kind strings instead of entity objects. Moment, arguments and cancel behaviour all differ. No key. |
| `living_damage_pre` | **Gap:** nearest `ServerLivingEntityEvents.ALLOW_DAMAGE`; also `AFTER_DAMAGE` | `ALLOW_DAMAGE` fires before mitigations with the living entity, source and a plain float amount, and a false answer cancels the hit ([living-api] lines 31-36; [living-mixin] lines 80-84); `AFTER_DAMAGE` fires at TAIL after damage or shield blocking, only if the entity is not dead or dying ([living-api] lines 46-56; [living-mixin] lines 84-92). `native_living_damage_pre` fires before the damage container is applied and gives the listener a mutable `DamageContainer` with no cancel answer, so neither event matches its arguments or contract. No key. |
| `living_destroy_block` | **Gap:** none | The event index has no entry for a mob destroying a block; its only `destroyBlock` entries are `PlayerBlockBreakEvents` and the client `ClientPlayerBlockBreakEvents`. `may_destroy` reads the level block state and calls `native_living_destroy_block` with the entity id, kind, position, state id and a cancel before the mob breaks the block. No Fabric callback runs at that moment. No key. |
| `living_get_projectile` | **Gap:** none | The event index has no `getProjectile`, ammunition or projectile-selection entry. `get_projectile` and `mob_bow_ammo` call `native_living_get_projectile` with a player UUID or mob id and kind, the weapon stack and a mutable optional projectile stack before the ammunition is consumed; Fabric offers no event with those arguments or that moment. No key. |
| `living_jump_power` | **Gap:** none; nearest `BlockEvents.USE_WITHOUT_ITEM` | `USE_WITHOUT_ITEM` is injected at HEAD of `BlockState.useWithoutItem` for a player interacting with a block and returns an `InteractionResult` ([block-events] lines 54-57, 90-104; [block-state-mixin] lines 46-54). `native_living_jump_power` is a loop-context motion query taking an entity id, kind and mutable `f32`, called from `adjust_jump_power` and `jump_power_with`; the name match is unrelated to the moment. No key. |
| `living_speed` | **Gap:** none | The event index has no movement-speed or `LivingEntity.getSpeed` entry. `native_living_speed` is a loop-context query with an entity id, kind and mutable `f32`, called for players and mobs on both sides and on replayed client ticks; Fabric has no motion-value callback at that moment. No key. |
| `living_flying_speed` | **Gap:** none | The same absence holds for `LivingEntity.getFlyingSpeed`: the index has no entry, and `native_living_flying_speed` is a loop-context query with an entity id, kind and mutable `f32` on both sides. No Fabric event runs there or carries those arguments. No key. |
| `living_shield_block` | **Gap:** nearest `ServerLivingEntityEvents.AFTER_DAMAGE` and `ALLOW_DAMAGE` | `ALLOW_DAMAGE` runs before shield resolution and only vetoes the hit, while `AFTER_DAMAGE` runs after it and merely reports the `blocked` flag ([living-api] lines 31-36, 46-56; [living-mixin] lines 80-92). `native_living_shield_block` fires where `applyItemBlocking` resolves the hit, before the amount is applied, and lets the listener set blocked, blocked damage and shield durability from ids and source data rather than the living entity object. No key. |
| `living_use_totem` | **Gap:** nearest `ServerLivingEntityEvents.ALLOW_DEATH` | `ALLOW_DEATH` is consulted from a redirect of `isDeadOrDying` in `hurtServer` with the living entity object, damage source and damage amount; its boolean return can veto death before totems take effect ([living-api] lines 140-150; [living-mixin] lines 75-78). `native_living_use_totem` runs inside `checkTotemDeathProtection`, only when a death-protection item is in hand, and its cancel drops the totem's protection so the entity still dies; it passes ids, a hand and an item id string instead of the living entity object, and adds hand and item data absent from Fabric. No key. |
| `living_visibility` | **Gap:** none | The event index has no visibility, targeting-range or `getVisibilityPercent` entry. `living_visibility` calls `native_living_visibility` with a `LivingRef`, an optional looking `EntityRef` and a mutable `f64` from the mob targeting path; no Fabric event fires at that moment or carries those arguments. No key. |

[mob-conversion-mixin]: https://github.com/FabricMC/fabric/blob/2065040b236a0d5ac011bd6a45625dc49579b222/fabric-entity-events-v1/src/main/java/net/fabricmc/fabric/mixin/entity/event/MobMixin.java
## Event counterparts: misc menu, mob spawn, split and config hooks (#1014)

Fabric evidence is at `2065040b236a0d5ac011bd6a45625dc49579b222` (`fabric-26.3/`); Horizon call sites are named by function, never by line. Scope: the eight hooks `menu_extra_data`, `minecart_pass`, `mob_despawn`, `mob_spawn_placement_check`, `mob_spawn_position_check`, `mob_split`, `register_configs` and `config_loading`. All eight record a gap and no keys are added.

| Horizon hook | Fabric event considered / status | Evidence and porting boundary |
|---|---|---|
| `menu_extra_data` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no menu-open event, and the event index has no `openMenu`, screen-handler or menu-extra-data entry. `open_block_menu_with_opener` calls `native_menu_extra_data` before the menu packet is sent, only for a mod menu type, with the player UUID, block and menu ids, the position and a mutable optional byte buffer that the first listener fills. No Fabric event runs at that moment or offers a replace answer over that data. No key. |
| `minecart_pass` | **Gap:** none | The event index has no minecart, rail or block-contact entry, and Fabric offers no per-entity block contact callback. `drain_entity_inside` drains a `Pending::MinecartPass` and calls `native_minecart_pass` with the block state id, the position and an entity ref after checking the level still holds that state. No Fabric event sits there or passes those arguments. No key. |
| `mob_despawn` | **Gap:** none; nearest `ServerEntityEvents.ENTITY_UNLOAD` | `ENTITY_UNLOAD` fires at tracking end, when an entity stops being tracked, and only reports the entity and server level ([sentity-api] line 67; [sentity-callbacks-mixin] lines 43-45). `native_mob_despawn` is called from `tick_creature_entities` before `checkDespawn`'s own draws, with the dimension, a `MobRef`, the kind, the position and a mutable `Despawn` verdict, and that verdict changes whether the mob despawns. Firing point, arguments and answer all differ. No key. |
| `mob_spawn_placement_check` | **Gap:** none; nearest `ServerEntityEvents.ENTITY_LOAD` | The event index has no spawn-placement, `checkSpawnRules` or `SpawnPlacementCheck` entry; `ENTITY_LOAD` fires at tracking start after an entity has joined the level ([sentity-api] line 43; [sentity-callbacks-mixin] lines 38-40). `placement_check` asks `native_mob_spawn_placement_check` with the dimension, kind, spawn reason, block position, the vanilla boolean and a mutable `SpawnCheck` answer, and a non-default answer replaces the type's rules. The vanilla placement result is already computed when Horizon asks the hook; a non-default answer replaces that result. Fabric `ALLOW_LOAD` instead vetoes `PersistentEntitySectionManager.addEntity` at HEAD with an entity, level, spawn reason and loaded-from-disk flag ([sentity-api] lines 50-58; [misc-menu-load-mixin] lines 34-41), so its firing point, arguments and veto-only answer differ. No key. |
| `mob_spawn_position_check` | **Gap:** none; nearest `ServerEntityEvents.ENTITY_LOAD` | `position_check` collects a mutable `SpawnCheck` with the dimension, kind, reason and centre position before vanilla's `checkSpawnRules` and `checkSpawnObstruction`, and `Succeed`/`Fail` skip both checks and their draws; `ALLOW_LOAD` is a veto at entity addition with entity data rather than a forced spawn-check verdict ([sentity-api] lines 50-58; [misc-menu-load-mixin] lines 34-41); `ENTITY_LOAD` is only a tracking-start notice after spawning ([sentity-api] line 43; [sentity-callbacks-mixin] lines 38-40). Firing point, arguments and answer differ. No key. |
| `mob_split` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no mob-split event and the event index has no split or `AbstractCubeMob` entry. `mob_split_count` calls `native_mob_split` with the parent entity id, kind, size, child size and mutable count and cancel after the child count is drawn, and a cancel or a changed count changes the children spawned. No Fabric event covers that moment. No key. |
| `register_configs` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 has no config registration event and the event index has no config-file entry; Fabric itself ships no config system to register with. `native_configs_load` calls `native_register_configs` to collect every mod's `Config` before reading the files for the requested kinds, then loads them. No Fabric event runs at that moment. No key. |
| `config_loading` | **Gap:** none | The event index has no config-load entry, and Fabric offers no callback when a mod's config file is read. `native_configs_load` calls `native_config_loading` once per file that `NativeConfigs::load` returned, before a `Server` file's world start. No Fabric event fires there or carries the config's owner, path and text. No key. |

[misc-menu-load-mixin]: https://github.com/FabricMC/fabric/blob/2065040b236a0d5ac011bd6a45625dc49579b222/fabric-lifecycle-events-v1/src/main/java/net/fabricmc/fabric/mixin/event/lifecycle/PersistentEntitySectionManagerMixin.java
## Event counterparts: misc config, model layer, input, note block and world notice hooks (#1014)

Fabric evidence is at `2065040b236a0d5ac011bd6a45625dc49579b222` (`fabric-26.3/`); Horizon call sites are named by function, never by line. Scope: the eight hooks `config_reloading`, `config_unloading`, `register_layer_definitions`, `movement_input`, `note_block_change`, `potential_spawns`, `village_siege` and `neighbor_notify`. All eight record a gap and no keys are added.

| Horizon hook | Fabric event considered / status | Evidence and porting boundary |
|---|---|---|
| `config_reloading` | **Gap:** none | Fabric API 26.3 declares no config-file event, the event index has no mod-config-file or `ModConfigEvent` entry, and Fabric ships no config system to fire one. `native_configs_poll` asks for files whose text changed on disk, at most once a second, and calls `native_config_reloading` per file with its new text; a file saved with the same text fires nothing. No Fabric event runs at that moment. No key. |
| `config_unloading` | **Gap:** none | The event index has no config-unload or `ModConfigEvent` entry either. `native_configs_server_stop` drops the server configs and calls `native_config_unloading` per dropped file after `native_server_stopping`; client and common configs stay until the game quits. No Fabric event fires there. No key. |
| `register_layer_definitions` | **Gap:** none; nearest `LivingEntityRenderLayerRegistrationCallback.EVENT` | That callback fires while each `EntityRendererProvider.create` result is built, only for a `LivingEntityRenderer`, and its registration helper only calls `addLayer` ([living-render-layer-api] lines 51-52; [entity-renderers-registration-mixin] lines 59-67). `register_layer_definitions` collects model-layer `(name, LayerDefinition)` pairs through `native_register_layer_definitions` into a `Vec` and registers them with `horizon_client::mobs::model_layers`, before any renderer exists. The moment, the registration stage and every argument differ; the callback offers neither the layer name nor the definition. No key. |
| `movement_input` | **Gap:** none | The event index has no movement-input or `MovementInputUpdateEvent` entry; its `ScreenKeyboardEvents` entries concern screen key handling, not mutable movement axes. `tick_local_player` reads local `Keys`, then `mod_movement_keys` calls `native_movement_input` with mutable forward, strafe and all seven independent key booleans. Direction-key edits replace the axes; otherwise axis edits update only the changed directions. Sprint requests obey vanilla eligibility and subscribed false suppresses sprint for the tick. No Fabric event fires there or offers those mutable inputs. No key. |
| `note_block_change` | **Gap:** none | The event index has no note-block, tuning or `NoteBlockEvent` entry. `note_block_change` calls `native_note_block_change` only on the authoritative server, with the dimension, position, instrument, old note, a mutable new note and a mutable cancel; cancel keeps the old note and prevents playback. No Fabric event covers that moment or those arguments. No key. |
| `potential_spawns` | **Gap:** none; nearest `EntitySleepEvents.ALLOW_SETTING_SPAWN` (name only) | `ALLOW_SETTING_SPAWN` merely vetoes `ServerPlayer.setRespawnPosition` when a player starts sleeping ([sleep-api] lines 170-175; [base-block-server-player-mixin] lines 102-110), so its moment, arguments and answer are unrelated. `potential_spawns` calls `native_potential_spawns` with the dimension, category, position, a mutable spawn-entry list and a mutable cancel during the natural spawn pass, and cancel empties the list. The event index has no spawn-pass or potential-spawn entry. No key. |
| `village_siege` | **Gap:** none | The event index has no village, siege or `VillageSiegeEvent` entry. `try_to_setup_siege` loops over non-spectator players in villages and, after drawing and finding a spawn position, calls `native_village_siege` with the dimension, player UUID, attempted position and a mutable cancel; cancel aborts the siege attempt. No Fabric event runs at that moment. No key. |
| `neighbor_notify` | **Gap:** none | The event index has no neighbour-notify, block-update or `NeighborNotifyEvent` entry. `neighbor_notify` runs on the authoritative server only when the hook is listened, reads the block state, and calls `native_neighbor_notify` with the dimension, position, state, skipped side and a mutable cancel, on both the all-six-sides and the skip-one-side update paths. No Fabric event fires per neighbour notification. No key. |

## Event counterparts: note block, item use, permissions, break speed, brewing, enchanting, fall, harvest, heart, smithing, name format and spawn hooks (#1014)

Fabric evidence is at `2065040b236a0d5ac011bd6a45625dc49579b222` (`fabric-26.3/`); Horizon call sites are named by function, never by line. Scope: the twelve hooks `note_block_play`, `on_item_use_first`, `permissions_changed`, `player_break_speed`, `player_brewed_potion`, `player_enchant_item`, `player_flyable_fall`, `player_harvest_check`, `player_heart_type`, `player_item_smithed`, `player_name_format` and `player_set_spawn`. All twelve record a gap and no keys are added.

| Horizon hook | Fabric event considered / status | Evidence and porting boundary |
|---|---|---|
| `note_block_play` | **Gap:** none | The event index has no note-block, tuning or `NoteBlockEvent` entry, the same absence recorded for `note_block_change`. `note_block_play` fires `native_note_block_play` on the authoritative server once the block above no longer mutes it, with the dimension, position, the player and entity id when a player tuned or punched it, a mutable instrument and note, and a mutable cancel that suppresses sound, particle and the game event. No Fabric event runs at that moment or offers those arguments. No key. |
| `on_item_use_first` | **Gap:** `UseBlockCallback.EVENT`; `BlockEvents.USE_ITEM_ON` | `UseBlockCallback.EVENT` runs before the spectator check ([useblock-api] lines 28-38; [interaction-server] lines 85-94), earlier than `item_use_first`, which runs after `use_item_on_block` (`ItemBeforeBlock`) and before the block's `useItemOn`. `BlockEvents.USE_ITEM_ON` instead intercepts `BlockState.useItemOn` on either side ([block-events-api] lines 37-53; [block-state-mixin] lines 41-48), accepts a player object rather than Horizon's player identity, and treats any non-null result, including `PASS`, as a replacement for the block call; Horizon runs server-side and `Pass` continues the click. The firing point, side, arguments and continuation contract do not all match. No key. |
| `permissions_changed` | **Gap:** nearest `PermissionEvents.ON_REQUEST` | `ON_REQUEST` ([permission-events-api] lines 45-50, listener line 92) answers a `PermissionNode` check through `PermissionContext`, and `PREPARE_OFFLINE_PLAYER` (lines 65-70, listener line 106) readies an offline context; the index finds no direct `invoker()` call for either, and neither is the operator-list write. `permission_change` runs when `PlayerList.op`/`deop` is about to give a player level `new` in place of `old`, with the UUID, optional entity id, name, both levels and a mutable cancel that keeps the old level. Different moment and arguments. No key. |
| `player_break_speed` | **Gap:** nearest `PlayerBlockBreakEvents.BEFORE` | `BEFORE` ([pbb-api] lines 36-44) is a server-only veto at `playerWillDestroy` inside `ServerPlayerGameMode.destroyBlock` ([interaction-server] lines 103-111), after the destroy speed has been computed, and it only cancels the break. `destroy_progress` computes the speed and calls `native_player_break_speed` on both sides with the entity id, position, block state, the original speed and a mutable speed plus cancel. This event carries no speed and does not run on the client. No key. |
| `player_brewed_potion` | **Gap:** none | The event index has no brewing, potion or take-from-slot entry. `player_brewed_potion` fires per stack taken from a brewing stand's bottle slot, on the server, with the player UUID, entity id and the stack. No Fabric event runs at that moment or provides the stack. No key. |
| `player_enchant_item` | **Gap:** nearest `EnchantmentEvents.MODIFY` | `ALLOW_ENCHANTING` gates an individual enchantment's applicability, whereas `MODIFY` and `MODIFY_WITH_LOOKUP` change enchantment definitions through a builder and resource source ([enchant-events-api] lines 54, 84 and 107; [enchant-util] lines 45-68), not the enchantments applied to a stack at a table. `lan_enchant` calls `native_player_enchant_item` after the levels are paid, with the enchanted stack and the ordered `(id, level)` list it was given. Different moment and arguments. No key. |
| `player_flyable_fall` | **Gap:** none | The event index has no fall, landing or `PlayerFlyableFallEvent` entry. A guest server tick turns `MotionEvent::Fall(FallEvent::FlyableFall)` into `native_player_flyable_fall` with the guest's UUID, entity id, name, distance and multiplier, because `Player.causeFallDamage` returned early for a mayfly player. No Fabric event fires at that moment. No key. |
| `player_harvest_check` | **Gap:** nearest `PlayerBlockBreakEvents.BEFORE` | `BEFORE` ([pbb-api] lines 36-44) is a server-only cancel at `playerWillDestroy` ([interaction-server] lines 103-111); it can stop the break but cannot change whether the tool harvests. `break_block_as_player` and `destroy_progress` call `native_player_harvest_check` on both sides with the entity id, position, block state and a mutable `can_harvest`, and use the answer for drops and speed. Different moment, side and argument. No key. |
| `player_heart_type` | **Gap:** none | The event index has no heart-type or `PlayerHeartTypeEvent` entry; its HUD entries only let a mod draw an element, not choose the heart sprites. `player_heart_kind` picks vanilla's poisoned, withered, frozen or normal kind and calls `native_player_heart_type` with a mutable kind while the HUD draws the hearts, before `health_hud` consumes it. No Fabric event offers that pick. No key. |
| `player_item_smithed` | **Gap:** none | The event index has no smithing, anvil or take-from-menu entry. `lan_station_op` drains `menu.item_smithed` and calls `native_player_item_smithed` with the player UUID, entity id, the template/base/addition inputs and the result taken from a smithing table. No Fabric event runs at that moment. No key. |
| `player_name_format` | **Gap:** none | The event index has no player-name-format or display-name entry. `native_player_name_format`, reached from `formatted_player_name`, `player_display_name` and `player_text`, runs while a chat line or command feedback is built, on the side `context.side()` names, with the username and a mutable display name. No Fabric event fires per formatted player name. No key. |
| `player_set_spawn` | **Gap:** nearest `EntitySleepEvents.ALLOW_SETTING_SPAWN` | `ALLOW_SETTING_SPAWN` only vetoes the `setRespawnPosition` call made from `startSleepInBed` ([sleep-api] lines 170-175; [base-block-server-player-mixin] lines 102-110), and it sees the sleeping player and sleeping position. `spawn_point_change` and `guest_respawn` fire once for every vanilla `setRespawnPosition` call (every sleep, a new anchor, `/spawnpoint`, the respawn copy) with the dimension, position, `forced` and a mutable cancel. One sleeping-only veto cannot stand for those calls. No key. |

[permission-events-api]: https://github.com/FabricMC/fabric/blob/2065040b236a0d5ac011bd6a45625dc49579b222/fabric-permission-api-v1/src/main/java/net/fabricmc/fabric/api/permission/v1/PermissionEvents.java
## Event counterparts: phantom spawn, ground surface, tab list name and experience hooks (#1014)

Fabric evidence is at `2065040b236a0d5ac011bd6a45625dc49579b222` (`fabric-26.3/`); Horizon call sites are named by function, never by line. Scope: the five hooks `player_spawn_phantoms`, `player_stays_on_ground_surface`, `player_tab_list_name_format`, `player_xp_change` and `player_xp_level_change`. All five record a gap and no keys are added.

| Horizon hook | Fabric event considered / status | Evidence and porting boundary |
|---|---|---|
| `player_spawn_phantoms` | **Gap:** none; nearest `EntitySleepEvents.ALLOW_SETTING_SPAWN` | The only name-adjacent event is `ALLOW_SETTING_SPAWN` ([sleep-api] lines 170-183 and 335), fired inside the `startSleepInBed` wrap that guards `setRespawnPosition` ([sp-events-mixin] lines 105-109). It vetoes a player's bed spawn, with a player object and a sleeping position, at a moment unrelated to phantom spawning. `player_spawn_phantoms` instead fires in `spawn_phantoms_near` for a non-spectator server player before the difficulty and rest rolls, after the group-size draw, with a mutable `phantoms` count and a three-way `result` (`Allow`/`Default`/`Deny`); nothing in Fabric runs there. No key. |
| `player_stays_on_ground_surface` | **Gap:** none | Horizon declares this vanilla `Player.isStayingOnGroundSurface` extension point itself: a `listen` hook on both sides, context `loop`, whose `neoforge` field records "none (vanilla Player.isStayingOnGroundSurface; NeoForge adds no event or extension for it)". The wrapper `native_player_stays_on_ground_surface` takes the loop context, the entity id and a mutable `staying`, and `staying_on_ground_surface` seeds it with the vanilla `shift_key_down` value. The event index has no event for that method, so there is no firing point, side or argument match to port. No key. |
| `player_tab_list_name_format` | **Gap:** none; nearest `CreativeModeTabEvents.MODIFY_OUTPUT_ALL` / `modifyOutputEvent(…)` | The candidates are creative-inventory events: `MODIFY_OUTPUT_ALL` and the per-tab `modifyOutputEvent` ([player-creative-tab-api] lines 41-75) modify entries of a `CreativeModeTab` at the tail of `buildContents` ([player-creative-tab-mixin] lines 55, 79-83). `player_tab_list_name_format` runs in `tab_list_name` while the Tab list packet is built, after the nickname default, and hands a player uuid and name plus a mutable optional `display_name`. Different moment and different data (tab contents versus a player's display name); neither event supplies a mutable player display name. No key. |
| `player_xp_change` | **Gap:** none | The event index has no player-experience entry; Horizon records NeoForge's `PlayerXpEvent.XpChange` counterpart. The hook fires in `xp_change` before the amount is applied, after the `listened` and acting-player guards, and passes the player uuid, entity id, name, a mutable signed `amount` and a mutable `cancel`. Fabric exposes nothing at `Player.giveExperiencePoints`, so neither the arguments nor the cancel contract can be matched. No key. |
| `player_xp_level_change` | **Gap:** none | The event index has no player-level-change entry; Horizon records NeoForge's `PlayerXpEvent.LevelChange` counterpart. The call site `add_experience_levels` returns on `levels == 0` and, when listened with an acting player, fires the hook with a mutable `levels` and a mutable `cancel` that aborts the change. The hook also documents the swapped-in player state used by `host.players().acting_player()` and the item helpers, and no Fabric event covers `Player.giveExperienceLevels`. No key. |

[player-creative-tab-mixin]: https://github.com/FabricMC/fabric/blob/2065040b236a0d5ac011bd6a45625dc49579b222/fabric-creative-tab-api-v1/src/main/java/net/fabricmc/fabric/mixin/creativetab/CreativeModeTabMixin.java
## Event counterparts: portal spawn, brewing, recipe, registration and rendering hooks (#1014)

The pinned Fabric revision is `2065040b236a0d5ac011bd6a45625dc49579b222` (`fabric-26.3/`); Horizon call sites are named by function, never by line. Scope: the twenty hooks in the `misc-portal` dossier, from `portal_spawn` to `render_tooltip_pre`. All twenty are gaps, so no `fabric` keys are added.

| Horizon hook | Fabric event considered / status | Evidence and porting boundary |
|---|---|---|
| `portal_spawn` | **Gap:** none; nearest `EntitySleepEvents.ALLOW_SETTING_SPAWN` | The only name-adjacent event is `ALLOW_SETTING_SPAWN` ([sleep-api] lines 175-183 and 335), fired inside the `startSleepInBed` wrap around `setRespawnPosition` ([sp-events-mixin] lines 105-109). It vetoes a player's bed spawn with a player object and sleeping position, whereas `portal_spawn` fires in `allow_portal_spawn`/`fire_portal_spawn` when a fire in an obsidian frame is about to form a portal, with `pos`, `state`, `shape` and a mutable `cancel`. Both are server-side vetoes, but the firing point and arguments differ. No key. |
| `potion_brew_post` | **Gap:** none | No Fabric event in the dossier or pinned event index covers `BrewingStandBlockEntity.doBrew` after the brew. Horizon's `tick_brewing_stand` fires `native_potion_brew_post` after `brewing_complete`, the brew sound and the ingredient remainder, with the stand's five mutable slots; Fabric has no event at that moment with those arguments. No key. |
| `potion_brew_pre` | **Gap:** none | No Fabric event in the dossier or pinned event index covers the start of `BrewingStandBlockEntity.doBrew`. Horizon's `tick_brewing_stand` fires `native_potion_brew_pre` before vanilla's brew with a five-slot `stacks` copy and a mutable `cancel`; no Fabric event shares that firing point, side or cancel behaviour. No key. |
| `pre_remove_side_effects` | **Gap:** none; nearest `ServerMobEffectEvents.AFTER_REMOVE` and `ALLOW_EARLY_REMOVE` | Both candidates are mob-effect removal events: `AFTER_REMOVE` is declared at [effect-api] line 143 and fired at [effect-mixin] lines 213-220, while `ALLOW_EARLY_REMOVE` is declared at lines 111 and 214 and fired at lines 122-126. They concern `LivingEntity.onEffectsRemoved` and `removeAllEffects`, not `BlockEntity.preRemoveSideEffects` or container drops. Horizon's `api_pre_remove_side_effects` collects `drops` for a mod block entity being removed; there is no Fabric counterpart. No key. |
| `recipe_book_categories` | **Gap:** none; nearest `ClientRecipeSynchronizedEvent.EVENT` | `ClientRecipeSynchronizedEvent.EVENT` ([recipe-sync-api] line 31) fires client-side after synchronized recipes are installed ([recipe-sync-impl] lines 52-56), with `Minecraft` and `SynchronizedRecipes`. `recipe_book_categories` is a both-side `listen` hook in `registered_recipe_book_category` that declares categories with item icons, before tabs or book displays exist. Its side, moment, arguments and contract do not match. No key. |
| `recipe_property_sets` | **Gap:** none; nearest `ClientRecipeSynchronizedEvent.EVENT` | The same event ([recipe-sync-api] line 31; [recipe-sync-impl] lines 52-56) is client-only and runs after recipe synchronization, so it cannot serve a both-side `data_load`-context collect hook that adds `RecipePropertyAddition`s after recipes resolve. Horizon's `native_recipe_property_sets` fills `additions` for `RecipeManager.RECIPE_PROPERTY_SETS`, not synchronized recipes. The firing point and arguments differ. No key. |

Horizon's `modding::mods::register_block_tint_sources` instead runs once at registration time to collect a `BlockTintRegistry` for meshing. It is not per-particle and has no cancel return, so the moment and contract differ. No key. |

Horizon's `modding::mods::register_debug_entries` calls `native_register_debug_entries` once to fill `entries`; there is no Fabric firing point, side and argument match. No key. |
| `register_debug_renderers` | **Gap:** none | No Fabric event in the dossier or pinned index corresponds to NeoForge's `RegisterDebugRenderersEvent` or Horizon's registration-time collect hook. Horizon's `modding::mods::debug_renderers` calls `native_register_debug_renderers` once to fill `renderers`; no Fabric event shares that moment or output shape. No key. |
| `register_gui_layers` | **Gap:** none; nearest `LivingEntityRenderLayerRegistrationCallback.EVENT` | That callback ([living-render-layer-api] line 52; listener line 65) fires when entity render layers are registered per `LivingEntityRenderer` ([entity-renderers-registration-mixin] lines 59-77), supplying entity type, renderer, registration helper and context. Horizon's `modding::mods::register_gui_layers` collects generic `GuiLayer` values once, not entity render layers, and the arguments and moment differ. No key. |

The hook is a client `listen` called by `render_and_flip` after `render()` with the frame's `elapsed` duration; Fabric has no event at that present/frame boundary in the evidence. No key. |

Horizon calls `native_render_frame_pre` from `extras_frame` just before the frame render with `elapsed`; no Fabric event shares that moment or argument. No key. |

No key. |

Horizon instead loops over its `RenderLevelStage` values in `fill_world_geometry` while collecting geometry, passing a stage, `WorldRenderView` and mutable `WorldGeometry`. Both are client-side notifications, but the firing points and arguments differ; one Horizon hook does not match the split Fabric events. No key. |

Horizon's `gather_tooltip_components` runs later, after `item_tooltip`, combines text and image elements, exposes `max_width` and `cancel`, and can suppress the tooltip; neither event matches. Horizon's `tooltip_pre` fires when a laid-out tooltip is about to be placed and drawn, passing position, screen size, read-only elements, a draw context and mutable `cancel`. ## Event counterparts: misc render, screen, sound, power and geometry hooks (#1014)

The pinned Fabric revision is `2065040b236a0d5ac011bd6a45625dc49579b222` (`fabric-26.3/`); Horizon call sites are named by function, never by line. Scope: the twenty hooks of the `misc-render` dossier, from `render_tooltip_texture` to `tick_target_millis`. All twenty are gaps, so no `fabric` keys are added.

| Horizon hook | Fabric event considered / status | Evidence and porting boundary |
|---|---|---|
| `render_tooltip_texture` | **Gap:** none; nearest `ItemTooltipCallback.EVENT`, `ClientTooltipComponentCallback.EVENT` | `ItemTooltipCallback` fires at the second RETURN of `ItemStack.getTooltipLines` ([item-tooltip-mixin] lines 37-41) after the base lines are appended, and hands `(stack, context, flag, lines)` ([item-tooltip-api] line 33) — line content, not box style. `ClientTooltipComponentCallback` only maps a `TooltipComponent` to a `ClientTooltipComponent` ([client-tooltip-component-api] line 37; fired at [client-tooltip-component-mixin] lines 34-38). Horizon fires in `tooltip_box` while the box is about to be drawn, with mutable `texture`, `background`, `border_top`, `border_bottom` and the described stack. Moment, arguments and effect differ. No key. |
| `right_click_empty` | **Gap:** none; nearest `ItemClickBehaviorCallback.EVENT`, `ScreenMouseEvents.afterMouseClick` | `ItemClickBehaviorCallback` is injected at the HEAD of `AbstractContainerMenu.tryItemClickBehaviourOverride` ([item-click-mixin] lines 36-40) for a slot click with the hovered and cursor stacks ([item-click-api] line 45), not a click into empty world space. `ScreenMouseEvents.afterMouseClick` is a per-screen event after a screen mouse click ([screen-mouse-events] line 70, listener line 215). Horizon's `remote_use_hands` calls `native_right_click_empty` only when nothing is targeted and the main hand is empty, client-side only. No key. |
| `screen_closing` | **Gap:** `ScreenEvents.remove` | Fabric passes the closed `Screen` ([screen-events] lines 106-115 and 195-196), after `Screen.removed()` in `Gui.setScreen` ([misc-render-gui-mixin] lines 66-68) or during shutdown ([misc-render-minecraft-mixin] lines 48-50). Horizon's `screen_change` calls `native_screen_closing` with a `ScreenView` before replacement, after opening was allowed. Both are client notices, but the firing point and screen arguments differ. No key. |
| `screen_exclusion_areas` | **Gap:** none; nearest `ClientReceiveMessageEvents.ALLOW_GAME`, `ClientReceiveMessageEvents.GAME` | Those fire when the client receives a game message ([receive-mixin] lines 69-75) to veto, modify or hear `(Component, overlay)` ([receive-api] lines 71 and 123). Horizon's `screen_exclusion` calls `native_screen_exclusion_areas` to collect `[x, y, width, height]` rectangles over the observed screen, after `screen_own_exclusion`. Neither the moment nor the arguments match. No key. |
| `screen_init_post` | **Gap:** `ScreenEvents.AFTER_INIT` | Fabric fires at TAIL of `Screen.init(II)` and `resize` ([misc-render-screen-mixin] lines 138-150 and 194-195), with Minecraft, the actual Screen and scaled dimensions ([screen-events] lines 99-103 and 189-191). Horizon fires `native_screen_init_post` from `open_screen_overlay`, `settle_title_mode` and `observe_change` for an observed opening or rectangle change, passing a `ScreenView`, at most once per frame. Both are client notices, but the observation moment and screen data differ from each completed init/resize. No key. |
| `screen_opening` | **Gap:** none | No Fabric candidate is listed for intercepting a screen replacement. `screen_change` calls `native_screen_opening` before the swap with the `current` and `new` views and a mutable `Answered<ScreenOpening>`, so a mod can cancel or force a mod screen. The dossier shows no Fabric `Gui.setScreen` event with that answer semantics. No key. |
| `screen_transparent_background` | **Gap:** none; nearest `ScreenEvents.afterBackground(…)` | `ScreenEvents.afterBackground` is a per-screen event after a screen's background was extracted ([screen-events] line 134, listener line 206; fired at [misc-render-screen-mixin] lines 121-123) and cannot stop it; it passes the `Screen` and a `GuiGraphicsExtractor`. Horizon's `render_transparent_background` calls `native_screen_transparent_background` first, before drawing, with the screen name string, the batch and a mutable `canceled`. Firing point and arguments differ, so the cancellation cannot be mapped. No key. |
| `screenshot` | **Gap:** none | The dossier has no Fabric candidate and no pinned event for a read-back screenshot. `save_screenshot` calls `native_screenshot` after the F2 frame was read back and before either PNG is written, with the RGBA image, mutable filename, optional message and mutable cancel. The hook names NeoForge's `ScreenshotEvent`; Fabric offers nothing at that moment. No key. |
| `should_check_weak_power` | **Gap:** none | The dossier lists no Fabric event for the weak-power query. `adjust_should_check_weak_power` calls `native_should_check_weak_power` with dimension, pos, state id and direction, seeding a mutable `check` from vanilla's conductor answer. No Fabric per-block extension or event is at that query with those arguments. No key. |
| `should_redstone_wire_connect_to` | **Gap:** none | The dossier lists no Fabric candidate, matching the hook's note that NeoForge adds nothing to vanilla `BlockBehaviour.shouldRedstoneWireConnectTo`. `adjust_should_redstone_wire_connect_to` passes dimension, pos, state id, an optional direction and a mutable `connected` seeded from vanilla. No Fabric event covers that query. No key. |
| `play_sound` | **Gap:** none | No Fabric event in the pinned event index covers `SoundEngine.play`. The hook target `horizon_client_render::sound_engine::hooks::native_play_sound` dispatches with the client loop context, instance id, starting flag, original sound and mutable optional sound before the engine checks; listeners can edit the sound or set it to `None`, including during live refreshes. The similarly named host method that queues authoritative world sounds is not this hook. No key. |
| `play_sound_source` | **Gap:** none; nearest `ClientPlayConnectionEvents.INIT`, `ClientPlayConnectionEvents.DISCONNECT` | The only candidates are the connection lifecycle events ([client-conn] lines 36 and 59; [play-addon] lines 51-55 and 95-99), which carry a `ClientPacketListener` and `Minecraft`, not a sound channel. `native_play_sound_source` (target `horizon_client_render::sound_engine::hooks`) reports a non-streamed sound after the engine set its pitch, volume, attenuation, looping and position. The dossier proves no Fabric client sound-engine event. No key. |
| `play_streaming_source` | **Gap:** none; nearest `ClientPlayConnectionEvents.INIT`, `ClientPlayConnectionEvents.DISCONNECT` | Again the listed candidates are connection INIT/DISCONNECT ([client-conn] lines 36 and 59; [play-addon] lines 51-55 and 95-99), unrelated to a streamed sound. `native_play_streaming_source` reports music or a record on a channel id after `play_sound`'s edits, and the channel gives no control. No Fabric streaming-source or sound event is proven in the dossier. No key. |
| `sound_engine_load` | **Gap:** none | The dossier lists no Fabric candidate for the sound engine opening its output. `native_sound_engine_load` fires at window start, after a resource reload with the new generation's loop context, and on an output-device change; nothing fires when there is no output. No Fabric event in the pinned index covers that moment. No key. |
| `spread_players_teleport` | **Gap:** none; nearest `ServerMessageEvents.ALLOW_COMMAND_MESSAGE`, `ServerMessageEvents.COMMAND_MESSAGE` | Those fire in `PlayerList.broadcastChatMessage` to veto or hear a broadcast command message ([player-list-mixin] lines 63-72) with message, source and chat type ([smsg-api] lines 84 and 127). `cmd_spreadplayers` calls `native_spread_players_teleport` once per entity after the spread computed `to`, with dimension, network id, optional player UUID, `from` and mutable `target`/`cancel`. Moment, arguments and cancel target differ. No key. |
| `submit_custom_geometry` | **Gap:** none; nearest `LevelRenderEvents.COLLECT_SUBMITS` | `COLLECT_SUBMITS` is injected at the RETURN of `LevelRenderer.submitFeatures` ([level-renderer-mixin] lines 89-93), after terrain is drawn and submit nodes are collected and before submit geometry is drawn, passing a `LevelRenderContext` ([level-render-events] lines 71 and 194). `fill_world_geometry` calls `native_submit_custom_geometry` after its own `RenderLevelStage` loop, at the last (`AfterLevel`) stage, with a `WorldRenderView` and its `WorldGeometry` buffer. The dossier does not prove the same vanilla moment, and the buffer is not Fabric's context/collector; the other candidate, `EntityElytraEvents.CUSTOM` ([elytra-api] line 49), is unrelated elytra flight. No key. |
| `sweep_attack` | **Gap:** none; nearest `AttackEntityCallback.EVENT`, `AttackBlockCallback.EVENT` | `AttackEntityCallback` fires on the client at the attack packet's send ([interaction-client] lines 132-136) with player, level, hand, entity and hit result ([attackentity-api] line 41), returning an `InteractionResult` that ends client processing. `AttackBlockCallback` ([attackblock-api] lines 47-48; fired at [interaction-client] lines 77-81) is a block attack, not a sweep. `sweep_decision` runs after `Player.is_sweep_attack` computed `vanilla`, passes the target only as an id string with mutable `sweeping`/`canceled`, and fires on the Game as well as the attacking client. Moment and arguments differ; Fabric also receives an entity object rather than the target type identifier. No key. |
| `teleport_command` | **Gap:** none; nearest `ClientSendMessageEvents.ALLOW_COMMAND`, `ClientSendMessageEvents.COMMAND` | Those fire on the client inside `ClientPacketListener.sendCommand` before a command is sent ([send-mixin] lines 46-52) and pass only the command string ([send-api] lines 58 and 120). `teleport_command_target` calls `native_teleport_command` on the authoritative server once per teleported entity, with dimension, network id, `from` and mutable `to`/`cancel`. Firing point, side and arguments differ. No key. |
| `texture_atlas_stitched` | **Gap:** none | The dossier lists no Fabric candidate for a stitch-and-publish moment. `texture_atlases_stitched` calls `native_texture_atlas_stitched` for the blocks then GUI atlases, on the first load and after every successful reload, before `client_setup`/`client_resources_reloaded`. No Fabric event in the dossier fires per atlas at that moment. No key. |
| `tick_target_millis` | **Gap:** none | The dossier lists no Fabric candidate, and the hook itself notes NeoForge has none. `tick_target_millis` seeds `millis` from the window clock and calls `native_tick_target_millis` once a frame before the fixed-step ticks, discarding non-finite or non-positive answers. No Fabric event covers the window's tick target. No key. |

## Event counterparts: client toasts, chunk-ticket validation, vanilla game events, wind bursts, window resizes and XP orb targeting (#1014)

Pinned Fabric revision `2065040b236a0d5ac011bd6a45625dc49579b222` (`fabric-26.3/`); Horizon is cited by function name, never by line. Scope: the six hooks of the `misc-toast` dossier, from `toast_add` to `xp_orb_targeting`. All six are gaps, so no `fabric` keys are added.

| Horizon hook | Fabric event considered / status | Evidence and porting boundary |
|---|---|---|
| `toast_add` | **Gap:** none | Horizon's `add_toast` and `native_toast_add` fire client-side just before a candidate toast joins the queue, handing `kind`, mutable `title`/`description` and a cancel answer that discards only this candidate, before slot allocation, animation or sound. The pinned Fabric event index has no toast-queue event, and no candidate is proven to fire at that moment, on that side, with those mutable components and that veto. No key. |
| `validate_tickets` | **Gap:** none | `validate_mod_chunk_tickets` runs server-side per controller after a level has loaded its saved chunk tickets, handing a `TicketValidation` that the owning mod mutates to drop stale block/entity tickets before the first tick. The pinned Fabric event index has no event for saved-ticket validation, so neither the per-controller moment nor the controller-scoped mutable validation object is matched. No key. |
| `vanilla_game_event` | **Gap:** none; nearest `ClientReceiveMessageEvents.ALLOW_GAME`, `ClientReceiveMessageEvents.GAME` | Both message events fire client-side at the HEAD of `ChatListener.handleSystemMessage` ([receive-mixin] lines 69-75) and carry a `Component` plus an overlay flag ([receive-api] lines 71 and 123; listener lines 184 and 234). Horizon's `post_game_event_with` and `native_vanilla_game_event` run server-side at `Level.gameEvent` with dimension, event id, position, cause and affected state, and cancelling silences sculk sensors, wardens and allays. Side, moment, arguments and effect all differ. No key. |
| `wind_burst` | **Gap:** none | `wind_burst` runs server-side after the explosion game event, the knockback and the shuffle, and offers the shuffled candidate block positions to `native_wind_burst` so positions can be removed, only when mob griefing lets a breeze interact with blocks. The pinned Fabric event index has no wind-burst or explosion event firing at that point with a mutable position list and no other change to knockback or random draws. No key. |
| `window_resize` | **Gap:** none | `notify_window_resize` fires client-side only when the physical or scaled dimensions change, passing physical width/height and scaled GUI width/height. The pinned Fabric event index has no window, fullscreen or GUI-scale event, and no candidate is proven to match that change-only moment with those four arguments. No key. |
| `xp_orb_targeting` | **Gap:** none | `orb_target` runs server-side on every orb tick, recomputes the nearest living non-spectator player within the scan distance, and lets `native_xp_orb_targeting` replace or clear the `following` player reference. The pinned Fabric event index has no experience-orb targeting event for that per-tick moment with an orb `EntityRef`, a scan distance and a mutable `PlayerRef`. No key. |
